# S10t: Теги (довідник, призначення, виключення з аналітики, фільтр). План реалізації

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Людина сама створює теги (назва, колір з палітри, перемикач «Не враховувати в аналітиці») і призначає їх операціям у формі, масово в журналі та автоправилом. Операція з тегом-прапорцем не потрапляє в Cash Flow, P&L і агрегати аналітики (`cash_*`, `cashcat_*`, `pl_*`), а лягає в паралельні `x_*`-документи; залишки рахунків не змінюються ніколи. Зміна прапорця перераховує минулі операції кроками з прогресом, після чого `verifyLedger` чистий.

**Architecture:** Чиста частина `lib/domain/tags/` (типи й ліміти, валідація входу, документ, ключ назви через `nameKey` контрагентів, правило `excludedFromTags`, об'єднання списків `mergeTags`, пошук для пікера). Ledger: `tags` стають id тегів (до 10), нове серверне поле `excluded`, `contributions` пише виключену операцію в `x_cash_*`, `x_cashcat_*`, `x_pl_*`; запис операції в тій самій транзакції читає всі її теги, перевіряє лише нові (`tag_not_found`, `tag_archived`) і обчислює `excluded`; `verifyLedger` звіряє `excluded`, `rebuildLedger` його перебудовує; `refreshExcluded` і `retagTransaction` теж живуть у `lib/server/ledger/write.ts` (єдине правило запису). Сервер `lib/server/tags/` пише `tags` і `tagKeys` в одній Admin-транзакції з `audit` (`tag.create|update`), рахує вплив і виконує крок перерахунку. Маршрути `app/api/orgs/[orgId]/tags/**`. Журнал: масові `addTagIds`/`removeTagIds`, фільтри `tg`/`ex`, чипи, колонка CSV. Автоправила S10: дія `tagIds` (додає, не підміняє). Браузер читає теги живою підпискою Firestore (`useTags`), розділ налаштувань «Теги», поле форми з створенням на місці, фільтр і масові дії журналу, поле «Додати теги» в редакторі правила.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16 route handlers, firebase-admin (транзакції, `array-contains`, `count()`), Firebase JS SDK 12 (`onSnapshot`), Vitest (unit + інтеграційні на емуляторі Firestore + правила Firestore), Playwright (емулятори + підроблений банк), next-intl (uk/pl/en).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-29-s10t-tags-design.md`

**Гілка:** `s10t-tags` (вже створена, на ній лежить спека). Усі коміти в неї. Кінець кожного повідомлення коміту: порожній рядок і `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Додавати файли поіменно (`git add <файли>`), ніколи `git add -A`.

**Робоче середовище (Windows, Git Bash):** репозиторій `F:\AI\finflow`. Для емулятора Firestore потрібна Java: `export PATH="/c/Users/Admin/.jre/temurin-21/bin:$PATH"`. Не вбивати процеси за ім'ям образу (`taskkill /IM node.exe` заборонено). Не робити junction на `node_modules`.

**Правила репозиторію, що діють у кожній задачі:** React Compiler lint (жодного синхронного `setState` у тілі ефекту: лише в колбеках промісів і обробниках); каталоги `messages/{uk,pl,en}.json` мають однакові ключі й однакові імена аргументів ICU (`tests/unit/i18n/catalogs.test.ts`); жодного кириличного тексту в коді `app/(app)`, `components`, `lib`, `types` (`tests/unit/i18n/no-hardcoded-text.test.ts`, коментарі можна; `placeholder`/`aria-label`/`title`/`alt` лише через `t()`); `new Intl.*` і `toLocale*()` лише в `lib/i18n` і `lib/utils`; `lib/server/**` імпортує `firebase-admin`, а клієнтський код ні; кожен маршрут під `app/api` викликає `await requireUser(req)` (`tests/unit/server/route-auth-coverage.test.ts`); кольори лише токенами дизайн-системи; `npm run lint` без нових попереджень (після S10 їх 10).

**Каталоги повідомлень:** файли `messages/*.json` записані рівно як `JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)` плюс перенос рядка (у робочій копії з CRLF). Тому кожен крок «Messages» нижче додає ключі детермінованим скриптом: тимчасовий файл `tmp-i18n.cjs` у корені репозиторію (створити інструментом Write, щоб кирилиця лишилась UTF-8), `node tmp-i18n.cjs`, потім `rm tmp-i18n.cjs`. Скрипт зливає фрагмент у каталог: новий ключ стає в кінець свого об'єкта, наявний ключ змінює значення на своєму місці, закінчення рядків файлу зберігаються. Порядок ключів не важить ні next-intl, ні тестам каталогів.

---

## Spec corrections

1. **Залишок після рядка журналу читає денні агрегати.** Спека: «`balanceEffects` від `excluded` не залежить: баланс рахунків завжди повний». Баланс рахунку справді не залежить, але залишок після кожного рядка журналу (`netsBetween` у `lib/server/journal/read.ts`) додає рух рахунку між днями сторінки з документів `cash_{рахунок}_{день}`. Виключена операція лягає в `x_cash_…`, тож без змін залишок після рядка став би хибним. План додає в `netsBetween` друге читання діапазону `x_cash_{рахунок}_…` і складає обидва (Task 7).
2. **Поточні сторінки Cash Flow і P&L рахують з операцій, а не з агрегатів.** Спека: виключені операції «не потрапляють у Cash Flow, P&L і дашборд», звіти з фільтром за тегами відкладено на S12. Нинішні сторінки `analytics/cashflow` і `analytics/pl` будують звіт у браузері з операцій (`buildCashFlow`, `buildPL` у `lib/utils/analytics.ts`), агрегатів не читають. Щоб мета спеки діяла вже в S10t, обидві функції пропускають операції з `excluded` (поле додається в UI-тип `Transaction`, Task 9). Календар і «Баланс» не змінюються. Фільтр за тегами у звітах лишається S12.

## Рішення плану (в межах спеки)

- **Браузер читає теги живою підпискою Firestore** (`lib/firebase/tags.ts`, `useTags`), як контрагентів: новий тег, створений з поля форми, і зняття `recalcPending` після перерахунку видно одразу. `GET /tags` є, як у спеці (для API і перевірок), але інтерфейс його не викликає.
- **Назва тегу:** обрізані краї, внутрішні пробіли згорнуто в один (як у контрагентів і правил), 1-50 символів, має бути хоч одна літера чи цифра (інакше немає ключа). Невідомий колір у документі читається як `slate`; некоректна назва чи прапорці роблять документ нечитабельним.
- **`excluded` зберігається лише як `true`**, відсутнє поле це `false`; із запиту поле не приймається (його немає в `CREATE_KEYS`, тож тіло з ним дає `400 invalid_input` з `field: 'excluded'`). Некоректне значення (не булеве) робить документ операції нечитабельним, і `verifyLedger` показує його як `uncountable`.
- **Запис операції читає всі її теги** (до 10 читань у тій самій транзакції), щоб обчислити `excluded` з прапорців, як вони є саме зараз; перевіряються (існує, не в архіві) лише теги, яких не було в попередньому стані. Тег, якого немає, вважається таким, що не виключає (як у `verifyLedger`).
- **Перерахунок** однієї операції це `refreshExcluded` у `lib/server/ledger/write.ts`: одна транзакція, `changeSet(prior, next)` (баланси за побудовою не рухаються), запис у `audit` як `tx.update` з `via: 'tag'` (правило S05: кожна зміна операції має запис журналу). Останній крок знімає `recalcPending` без запису в `audit` (службова позначка; дію людини вже записав `PATCH`).
- **Масова зміна тегів** не змішується з іншими полями в одному `patch` (`400 invalid_input` на `patch`); для кожної операції `retagTransaction` читає її, рахує новий список (`mergeTags`: спершу знімає, потім додає за порядком, понад 10 нові не додаються, як у правил) і пише через `updateTransaction` з `expectVersion` (зміна між читанням і записом дає `conflict` для цього рядка). Список без змін не пишеться і рахується як `ok`.
- **Дія правила `tagIds`** неробоча цілком, якщо хоч один її тег відсутній чи в архіві (як каже спека про «дію»); `PATCH` правила перевіряє лише нові теги дії, тож неробоче правило можна вимкнути чи перейменувати. «Перевірити» рахує кандидатом операцію без категорії чи контрагента або таку, якій бракує хоч одного тегу правила (і є місце до 10).
- **Фільтр журналу:** `tg` це id через кому, спеціальне значення `_none` означає «без тегів» (id тегів це авто-id Firestore, `_none` з ними не збігається); `ex=1` лишає тільки виключені. Обидва працюють у пам'яті (`needsScan`), нових індексів немає.
- **Кольори** палітри це токени `--color-tag-*` в `app/globals.css` (утиліти `bg-tag-*`), не hex у компонентах.
- **Порядок розділів налаштувань:** «Теги» після «Контрагенти», перед «Правила» (правила посилаються на теги).
- **Тексти з кількістю** записано через двокрапку («Буде перераховано операцій: {count}»), як у S10, без ICU-множин.
- **`check:tags`** використовує той самий розбір аргументів, що `check:journal` (`scripts/lib/check-journal-args.ts`, вже покритий тестами), а свою чисту логіку тримає в `scripts/lib/check-tags.ts` з тестом.

### Task 1: Чиста логіка тегів `lib/domain/tags/`

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/domain/tags/types.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/errors.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/guards.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/input.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/doc.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/keys.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/exclude.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/assign.ts`, `lib/domain/tags/search.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/domain/tags/input.test.ts`, `tests/unit/domain/tags/doc.test.ts`, `tests/unit/domain/tags/exclude.test.ts`, `tests/unit/domain/tags/assign.test.ts`, `tests/unit/domain/tags/search.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Створити `tests/unit/domain/tags/input.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { TagError } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import { cleanTagName, parseRecalcRequest, parseTagInput, parseTagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'

function code(fn: () => unknown): string {
  try {
    fn()
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof TagError ? `${e.code}:${e.field}` : 'other'
  }
  return 'none'
}

describe('parseTagInput', () => {
  it('fills the colour and the flag', () => {
    expect(parseTagInput({ name: '  ПДВ  ' })).toEqual({ name: 'ПДВ', color: 'slate', excludeFromAnalytics: false })
  })

  it('keeps a palette colour and the flag, and collapses inner spaces', () => {
    expect(parseTagInput({ name: 'Bankomat   kasa', color: 'amber', excludeFromAnalytics: true })).toEqual({
      name: 'Bankomat kasa',
      color: 'amber',
      excludeFromAnalytics: true,
    })
  })

  it('accepts a name of exactly 50 characters', () => {
    expect(parseTagInput({ name: 'x'.repeat(50) }).name).toHaveLength(50)
  })

  it.each([
    ['a non-object body', 'x', 'invalid_input:body'],
    ['an unknown key', { name: 'a', archived: true }, 'invalid_input:archived'],
    ['no name', {}, 'invalid_input:name'],
    ['an empty name', { name: '   ' }, 'invalid_input:name'],
    ['a name of 51 characters', { name: 'x'.repeat(51) }, 'invalid_input:name'],
    ['a name without a letter or digit', { name: '!!!' }, 'invalid_input:name'],
    ['a colour outside the palette', { name: 'a', color: '#ff0000' }, 'invalid_input:color'],
    ['a flag that is not a boolean', { name: 'a', excludeFromAnalytics: 'yes' }, 'invalid_input:excludeFromAnalytics'],
  ])('refuses %s', (_label, body, expected) => {
    expect(code(() => parseTagInput(body))).toBe(expected)
  })
})

describe('parseTagPatch', () => {
  it('takes only what is given', () => {
    expect(parseTagPatch({ archived: true })).toEqual({ archived: true })
    expect(parseTagPatch({ name: ' VAT ', color: 'rose', excludeFromAnalytics: false })).toEqual({ name: 'VAT', color: 'rose', excludeFromAnalytics: false })
  })

  it.each([
    ['an empty patch', {}, 'invalid_input:body'],
    ['recalcPending from a client', { recalcPending: false }, 'invalid_input:recalcPending'],
    ['an archived flag that is not a boolean', { archived: 1 }, 'invalid_input:archived'],
    ['an empty name', { name: '' }, 'invalid_input:name'],
  ])('refuses %s', (_label, body, expected) => {
    expect(code(() => parseTagPatch(body))).toBe(expected)
  })
})

describe('parseRecalcRequest', () => {
  it('a first step has no cursor, a next one names the last operation it read', () => {
    expect(parseRecalcRequest({})).toEqual({ cursor: null })
    expect(parseRecalcRequest({ cursor: 'tx-123' })).toEqual({ cursor: 'tx-123' })
  })

  it('refuses a cursor that is not a document id and unknown keys', () => {
    expect(code(() => parseRecalcRequest({ cursor: 'a/b' }))).toBe('invalid_input:cursor')
    expect(code(() => parseRecalcRequest({ step: 1 }))).toBe('invalid_input:step')
  })
})

describe('cleanTagName', () => {
  it('is the cleaning both parsers use', () => {
    expect(cleanTagName(' a   b ')).toBe('a b')
  })
})
```

```ts
/** The palette of tag colours (S10t): keys, never colour values. The UI maps them to the `--color-tag-*` tokens. */
export const TAG_COLORS = ['slate', 'sky', 'emerald', 'amber', 'rose', 'violet'] as const
export type TagColor = (typeof TAG_COLORS)[number]
export const DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR: TagColor = 'slate'

/** Characters of a tag name. */
export const TAG_NAME_MAX = 50
/** Tags one company may have (a technical limit; plan limits arrive in S17). */
export const MAX_TAGS = 100
/** Tags one operation may carry. */
export const MAX_TX_TAGS = 10
/** Tags one rule may add. */
export const MAX_RULE_TAGS = 5
/** Operations one step of `POST /tags/{id}/recalculate` reads. */
export const RECALC_PER_STEP = 100

export interface Tag {
  id: string
  name: string
  color: TagColor
  /** An operation with at least one such tag stays out of Cash Flow, P&L and their rollups; its balances do not change. */
  excludeFromAnalytics: boolean
  /** Stays on the operations that have it (muted); a new assignment is refused. */
  archived: boolean
  /** True while the operations of the tag are recalculated after a change of the flag. */
  recalcPending: boolean
}

/** `PATCH /tags/{id}`: the tag after the change, and whether its operations must be recalculated now. */
export interface UpdateTagResult {
  tag: Tag
  recalc: boolean
}

/** `GET /tags/{id}/impact`: the operations that carry the tag. */
export interface TagImpact {
  operations: number
}

/** One step of `POST /tags/{id}/recalculate`. */
export interface RecalcStepResult {
  /** Operations with the tag this step read. */
  scanned: number
  /** Of those, the ones whose `excluded` changed (their rollups moved). */
  changed: number
  done: boolean
  /** The id of the last operation read: send it back for the next step. Null when done. */
  cursor: string | null
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/errors.ts`:

```ts
export type TagErrorCode = 'invalid_input' | 'not_found' | 'duplicate' | 'limit_reached'

/**
 * An expected failure of a tag operation. `field` names the offending input field (`invalid_input`, `duplicate`);
 * `existingId` is the tag that already owns the name.
 */
export class TagError extends Error {
  public readonly code: TagErrorCode
  public readonly field?: string
  public readonly existingId?: string

  constructor(code: TagErrorCode, detail: { field?: string; existingId?: string } = {}) {
    super(detail.field === undefined ? code : `${code}:${detail.field}`)
    this.name = 'TagError'
    this.code = code
    this.field = detail.field
    this.existingId = detail.existingId
  }
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/guards.ts`:

```ts
import { TagError } from './errors'

export function bad(field: string): never {
  throw new TagError('invalid_input', { field })
}

export function asRecord(v: unknown, field = 'body'): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null || Array.isArray(v)) bad(field)
  return v as Record<string, unknown>
}

export function onlyKeys(r: Record<string, unknown>, allowed: readonly string[]): void {
  for (const k of Object.keys(r)) if (!allowed.includes(k)) bad(k)
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/input.ts`:

```ts
import { nameKeyText } from '@/lib/domain/counterparties/normalize'
import { isId } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { asRecord, bad, onlyKeys } from './guards'
import { DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR, TAG_COLORS, TAG_NAME_MAX, type TagColor } from './types'

/** A validated create request (defaults filled). */
export interface TagInput {
  name: string
  color: TagColor
  excludeFromAnalytics: boolean
}

/** A validated PATCH: only the fields given, at least one. */
export interface TagPatch {
  name?: string
  color?: TagColor
  excludeFromAnalytics?: boolean
  archived?: boolean
}

/** The display name: trimmed, inner whitespace collapsed, 1-50 characters, with a letter or a digit (it has no key otherwise). */
export function cleanTagName(v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string') bad('name')
  const text = v.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
  if (text.length < 1 || text.length > TAG_NAME_MAX || nameKeyText(text) === '') bad('name')
  return text
}

function color(v: unknown): TagColor {
  if (!(TAG_COLORS as readonly unknown[]).includes(v)) bad('color')
  return v as TagColor
}

function flag(v: unknown, field: string): boolean {
  if (typeof v !== 'boolean') bad(field)
  return v
}

export function parseTagInput(body: unknown): TagInput {
  const r = asRecord(body)
  onlyKeys(r, ['name', 'color', 'excludeFromAnalytics'])
  return {
    name: cleanTagName(r.name),
    color: r.color === undefined ? DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR : color(r.color),
    excludeFromAnalytics: r.excludeFromAnalytics === undefined ? false : flag(r.excludeFromAnalytics, 'excludeFromAnalytics'),
  }
}

export function parseTagPatch(body: unknown): TagPatch {
  const r = asRecord(body)
  onlyKeys(r, ['name', 'color', 'excludeFromAnalytics', 'archived'])
  const out: TagPatch = {}
  if (r.name !== undefined) out.name = cleanTagName(r.name)
  if (r.color !== undefined) out.color = color(r.color)
  if (r.excludeFromAnalytics !== undefined) out.excludeFromAnalytics = flag(r.excludeFromAnalytics, 'excludeFromAnalytics')
  if (r.archived !== undefined) out.archived = flag(r.archived, 'archived')
  if (Object.keys(out).length === 0) bad('body')
  return out
}

/** `POST /tags/{id}/recalculate`: the id of the last operation the previous step read (absent on the first one). */
export function parseRecalcRequest(body: unknown): { cursor: string | null } {
  const r = asRecord(body)
  onlyKeys(r, ['cursor'])
  if (r.cursor === undefined) return { cursor: null }
  if (!isId(r.cursor)) bad('cursor')
  return { cursor: r.cursor }
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/doc.ts`:

```ts
import { DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR, TAG_COLORS, type Tag, type TagColor } from './types'

export const TAG_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1

/** The stored fields of a tag (no id, timestamps or orgId: the writer adds those). */
export function tagToDoc(tag: Tag): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    schemaVersion: TAG_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    name: tag.name,
    color: tag.color,
    excludeFromAnalytics: tag.excludeFromAnalytics,
    archived: tag.archived,
    recalcPending: tag.recalcPending,
  }
}

function isRecord(v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v)
}

/**
 * Strict on what the ledger depends on (the name and the two flags), lenient on looks: a colour outside the palette
 * reads as the default one. Null for a document that is not a schema 1 tag.
 */
export function parseTag(id: string, data: unknown): Tag | null {
  if (!isRecord(data) || data.schemaVersion !== TAG_SCHEMA_VERSION) return null
  const { name, color, excludeFromAnalytics, archived, recalcPending } = data
  if (typeof name !== 'string' || name === '') return null
  if (typeof excludeFromAnalytics !== 'boolean' || typeof archived !== 'boolean') return null
  return {
    id,
    name,
    color: (TAG_COLORS as readonly unknown[]).includes(color) ? (color as TagColor) : DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR,
    excludeFromAnalytics,
    archived,
    recalcPending: recalcPending === true,
  }
}
```

```ts
import { nameKey } from '@/lib/domain/counterparties/normalize'

/**
 * Id of the uniqueness key document of a tag name (`tagKeys/name_…`): the counterparty name key, so case and Latin
 * diacritics do not count (`Łódź` = `lodz`) and Cyrillic letters keep their identity.
 */
export const tagNameKey = (name: string): string => nameKey(name)
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/exclude.ts`:

```ts
/** What the `excluded` flag of an operation needs to know about one tag (S10t). */
export interface TagFlags {
  excludeFromAnalytics: boolean
}

/** A tag document as a reader sees it (a Firestore snapshot fits): its id, whether it exists, its fields. */
export interface TagFlagSource {
  id: string
  exists: boolean
  get(field: string): unknown
}

/** The rule of S10t: an operation is excluded when at least one of its tags has the flag. A tag that does not exist does not exclude. */
export function excludedFromTags(tagIds: readonly string[], tags: ReadonlyMap<string, TagFlags>): boolean {
  return tagIds.some((id) => tags.get(id)?.excludeFromAnalytics === true)
}

/** The flags of the tag documents that exist, by id; anything but `true` is no flag. */
export function tagFlagsOf(docs: readonly TagFlagSource[]): Map<string, TagFlags> {
  const out = new Map<string, TagFlags>()
  for (const d of docs) if (d.exists) out.set(d.id, { excludeFromAnalytics: d.get('excludeFromAnalytics') === true })
  return out
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/assign.ts`:

```ts
import { isId } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { MAX_TX_TAGS } from './types'

/** What a bulk change does to the tags of each operation (S10t). */
export interface TagChange {
  add: readonly string[]
  remove: readonly string[]
}

/** The tag ids of a request: an array of at most `max` safe document ids, none twice; null when it is not one. */
export function tagIdsOf(v: unknown, max: number): string[] | null {
  if (!Array.isArray(v) || v.length > max) return null
  if (!v.every((id) => isId(id)) || new Set(v).size !== v.length) return null
  return [...(v as string[])]
}

/**
 * The tags after a change: the current ones without `remove`, then the ones of `add` that are not there yet, in their
 * order, while there is room. Never more than `max` (what is already there is never dropped), never a repeat.
 */
export function mergeTags(current: readonly string[], add: readonly string[], remove: readonly string[] = [], max: number = MAX_TX_TAGS): string[] {
  const out = current.filter((id) => !remove.includes(id))
  for (const id of add) {
    if (out.length >= max) break
    if (!out.includes(id)) out.push(id)
  }
  return out
}

/** The same ids in the same order. */
export function sameTags(a: readonly string[], b: readonly string[]): boolean {
  return a.length === b.length && a.every((id, i) => id === b[i])
}
```

Створити `lib/domain/tags/search.ts`:

```ts
import { nameKeyText } from '@/lib/domain/counterparties/normalize'
import type { Tag } from './types'

/** Every list shows the tags in this order: by the comparable name (case and Latin diacritics ignored), then by id. A new array. */
export function sortTags<T extends Pick<Tag, 'id' | 'name'>>(tags: readonly T[]): T[] {
  return [...tags].sort((a, b) => {
    const x = nameKeyText(a.name)
    const y = nameKeyText(b.name)
    if (x !== y) return x < y ? -1 : 1
    return a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0
  })
}

/**
 * The tags a picker offers for what was typed: not chosen yet, not archived (unless `includeArchived`, for taking a tag
 * off), whose name contains the typed text the way the uniqueness key compares names. In the order of `sortTags`.
 */
export function pickableTags(tags: readonly Tag[], query: string, chosen: readonly string[], opts: { includeArchived?: boolean } = {}): Tag[] {
  const q = nameKeyText(query)
  return sortTags(
    tags.filter((t) => !chosen.includes(t.id) && (opts.includeArchived === true || !t.archived) && (q === '' || nameKeyText(t.name).includes(q))),
  )
}

/** The tag that already owns this name (the same key as on the server, archived tags included), if any. */
export function tagByName(tags: readonly Tag[], name: string): Tag | undefined {
  const key = nameKeyText(name)
  return key === '' ? undefined : tags.find((t) => nameKeyText(t.name) === key)
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { changeSet } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/change'
import { parseTransaction, transactionToDoc } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/doc'
import { balanceEffects } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/effects'
import { cashCatDocId, cashDocId, contributions, EXCLUDED_PREFIX, excludedDocId, plDocId, type RollupDocs } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/rollups'
import type { LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { withExcluded } from '@/lib/domain/tags/exclude'

const tx = (over: Partial<LedgerTransaction> = {}): LedgerTransaction => ({
  id: 't', type: 'expense', status: 'actual', accountId: 'a', amountMinor: 1000, currency: 'PLN',
  paymentDate: '2026-10-20', accrual: { mode: 'date', date: '2026-10-20' }, categoryId: 'cat', isRecurring: false, tags: ['vat'], fileUrls: [], ...over,
})

const plain = (docs: RollupDocs) => Object.fromEntries(docs)

describe('an operation excluded from analytics', () => {

    expect(EXCLUDED_PREFIX).toBe('x_')

    expect(plain(contributions(tx({ excluded: true }), 'PLN'))).toEqual({

    expect(plain(contributions(t, 'PLN'))).toEqual({
      'x_cash_a_2026-10-20': { transferOutMinor: 1000 },
      'x_cash_b_2026-10-20': { transferInMinor: 1000 },
    })

  it('switching the flag moves the rollups between the twins and leaves the balances alone', () => {
    const change = changeSet(tx(), tx({ excluded: true }), 'PLN')
    expect(change.balanceDeltas).toEqual({})
    expect(plain(change.rollupDeltas)).toEqual({
      [cashDocId('a', '2026-10-20')]: { outMinor: -1000, outBase: -1000 },
      [cashCatDocId('2026-10')]: { 'categories.cat.expenseBase': -1000 },
      [plDocId('2026-10')]: { 'categories.cat.expenseBase': -1000 },
      'x_cash_a_2026-10-20': { outMinor: 1000, outBase: 1000 },
      'x_cashcat_2026-10': { 'categories.cat.expenseBase': 1000 },
      'x_pl_2026-10': { 'categories.cat.expenseBase': 1000 },
    })
  })
})

describe('the excluded field of a document', () => {
  it('is stored only when true and read back', () => {
    expect(transactionToDoc(tx()).excluded).toBeUndefined()
    expect(transactionToDoc(tx({ excluded: true })).excluded).toBe(true)
    expect(parseTransaction('t', transactionToDoc(tx({ excluded: true })))).toEqual(tx({ excluded: true }))
  })

  it('false or absent reads as not excluded (operations written before S10t)', () => {
    expect(parseTransaction('t', { ...transactionToDoc(tx()), excluded: false })).toEqual(tx())
    expect(parseTransaction('t', transactionToDoc(tx()))?.excluded).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it('a value that is not a boolean makes the document unreadable', () => {
    expect(parseTransaction('t', { ...transactionToDoc(tx()), excluded: 'yes' })).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('withExcluded', () => {
  it('sets the flag, or removes the key', () => {
    expect(withExcluded(tx(), true)).toEqual(tx({ excluded: true }))
    expect(withExcluded(tx({ excluded: true }), false)).toEqual(tx())
    expect('excluded' in withExcluded(tx({ excluded: true }), false)).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

на:

```ts
import { computeLedger } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/expected'
import { addInto, diffRollups, EXCLUDED_PREFIX, type RollupDocs } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/rollups'
import { withExcluded } from '@/lib/domain/tags/exclude'
```

після функції `checkAgainstOracle` додати:

```ts
/** The x_ twins added back into their analytics documents: the rollups as they would be if nothing were excluded. */
function foldExcluded(docs: RollupDocs): RollupDocs {
  const out: RollupDocs = new Map()
  for (const [id, flat] of docs) addInto(out, new Map([[id.startsWith(EXCLUDED_PREFIX) ? id.slice(EXCLUDED_PREFIX.length) : id, flat]]))
  return out
}
```

і в кінець `describe('ledger algebra ...')` (перед його закривальним `})`) додати:

```ts
  it('the excluded flag never moves a balance: without it the same operations give the same balances, and the x_ twins fold back', () => {
    let excludedDocs = 0
    for (let seed = 1; seed <= 100; seed++) {
      const r = rng(9000 + seed)
      const flagged = new Model()
      const plain = new Model()
      for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
        const tx = randomTx(r, `t${i}`)
        if (!tx) continue
        flagged.commit(null, tx)
        plain.commit(null, withExcluded(tx, false))
      }
      expect(flagged.balances).toEqual(plain.balances)
      expect(diffRollups(foldExcluded(flagged.rollups), plain.rollups)).toEqual([])
      for (const id of plain.rollups.keys()) expect(id.startsWith(EXCLUDED_PREFIX)).toBe(false)
      excludedDocs += [...flagged.rollups.keys()].filter((id) => id.startsWith(EXCLUDED_PREFIX)).length
    }
    expect(excludedDocs).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })
```

```ts
import { tagIdsOf } from '@/lib/domain/tags/assign'
```

після функції `stringList` додати:

```ts
/** The tag ids of an operation (S10t): at most 10 safe document ids, none twice; `[]` when absent. */
function tagList(v: unknown): string[] {
  if (v === undefined) return []
  const ids = tagIdsOf(v, MAX_TX_TAGS)
  if (ids === null) bad('tags')
  return ids
}
```

`lib/domain/ledger/rollups.ts`: після рядка з `export const plDocId` (оголошення id документа `pl_{місяць}`) додати:

```ts
/** The prefix of the twins of the rollup documents that hold the operations excluded from analytics (S10t). */
export const EXCLUDED_PREFIX = 'x_'
/** `x_cash_…`, `x_cashcat_…`, `x_pl_…`: the same fields as the analytics document `id`, for the excluded operations. */
export const excludedDocId = (id: string) => `${EXCLUDED_PREFIX}${id}`
```

і замінити doc-коментар та всю функцію `contributions` на:

```ts
/**
 * What one operation adds to the rollups (times `sign`):
 *  - cash_{account}_{paymentDay}: in/out in the account currency and in the company currency; transfer in/out;
 *  - cashcat_{paymentMonth}: per category, company currency;
 *  - pl_{accrualMonth}: per category, company currency, a period spread over its months.
 * An operation excluded from analytics (S10t: one of its tags has the flag) adds the same fields to the `x_` twins of
 * those documents instead, so the analytics leave it out; its balance effects (`balanceEffects`) never look at the flag.
 * Planned operations contribute nothing. Throws when an operation in another currency has no base amounts.
 */
export function contributions(tx: LedgerTransaction, baseCurrency: string, sign: 1 | -1 = 1): RollupDocs {
  const docs: RollupDocs = new Map()
  if (tx.status !== 'actual') return docs
  const doc = (id: string): string => (tx.excluded === true ? excludedDocId(id) : id)

  if (tx.type === 'transfer') {
    bump(docs, doc(cashDocId(tx.accountId, tx.paymentDate)), 'transferOutMinor', sign * tx.amountMinor)
    bump(docs, doc(cashDocId(tx.toAccountId as string, tx.paymentDate)), 'transferInMinor', sign * (tx.toAmountMinor as number))
    return docs
  }

  const base = baseOf(tx, baseCurrency)
  if (!base) throw new Error(`operation ${tx.id} has no base amounts`)
  const income = tx.type === 'income'
  const category = tx.categoryId ?? NO_CATEGORY
  const field = `categories.${category}.${income ? 'incomeBase' : 'expenseBase'}`

  const cash = doc(cashDocId(tx.accountId, tx.paymentDate))
  bump(docs, cash, income ? 'inMinor' : 'outMinor', sign * tx.amountMinor)
  bump(docs, cash, income ? 'inBase' : 'outBase', sign * base.payment)
  bump(docs, doc(cashCatDocId(monthOf(tx.paymentDate))), field, sign * base.payment)

  const parts: Array<[string, number]> =
    tx.accrual.mode === 'date'
      ? [[monthOf(tx.accrual.date), base.accrual]]
      : allocatePeriod(base.accrual, tx.accrual.from, tx.accrual.to)
  for (const [month, value] of parts) bump(docs, doc(plDocId(month)), field, sign * value)
  return docs
}
```

`lib/domain/tags/exclude.ts`: першим рядком файлу додати імпорт

```ts
import type { LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
```

і в кінець файлу додати:

```ts
/** The operation with `excluded` set (true) or without the key (false): the ledger stores the flag only when it is on. */
export function withExcluded(tx: LedgerTransaction, excluded: boolean): LedgerTransaction {
  const { excluded: _old, ...rest } = tx
  void _old
  return excluded ? { ...rest, excluded: true } : rest
}
```

Створити `tests/integration/tags-write.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { parseTransactionInput } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import { TagError } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import { parseTagInput, parseTagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { createTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { listTags, tagImpact } from '@/lib/server/tags/read'
import { createTag, updateTag } from '@/lib/server/tags/write'
import { ORG, UID, auditActions, clearFirestore, makeDeps, readDoc, seedAccount, seedOrg, testDb } from './helpers'

const db = testDb()
const deps = makeDeps(db)

async function code(p: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> {
  try {
    await p
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof TagError ? `${e.code}:${e.field ?? ''}:${e.existingId ?? ''}` : `other:${String(e)}`
  }
  return 'none'
}

const tagged = (tagId: string) =>
  createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, parseTransactionInput({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'a1', amountMinor: 100, paymentDate: '2026-10-20', tags: [tagId] }))

beforeEach(async () => {
  await clearFirestore()
  await seedOrg(db)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'a1', 'PLN', 0)
})

describe('createTag', () => {
  it('writes the tag, its name key and the audit entry together', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ', excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
    expect(tag).toEqual({ id: expect.any(String), name: 'ПДВ', color: 'slate', excludeFromAnalytics: true, archived: false, recalcPending: false })
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${tag.id}`)).toMatchObject({ schemaVersion: 1, name: 'ПДВ', orgId: ORG, createdBy: UID })
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tagKeys/name_пдв`)).toEqual({ tagId: tag.id })
    expect(await auditActions(db, ORG)).toEqual(['tag.create'])
  })

  it('refuses a name another tag has (case and Latin diacritics ignored) and names that tag', async () => {
    const first = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'Łódź' }))
    expect(await code(createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'lodz' })))).toBe(`duplicate:name:${first.id}`)
    expect((await db.collection(`organizations/${ORG}/tags`).get()).size).toBe(1)
  })

  it('refuses the tag over the limit in the same transaction', async () => {
    const small = { ...deps, maxCount: 2 }
    await createTag(small, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'a' }))
    await createTag(small, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'b' }))
    expect(await code(createTag(small, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'c' })))).toBe('limit_reached::')
  })

  it('refuses an unknown company', async () => {
    expect(await code(createTag(deps, 'ghost', UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'a' })))).toBe('not_found::')
  })
})

describe('updateTag', () => {
  it('a rename moves the name key; the old name is free again', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'VAT' }))
    const out = await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ name: 'ПДВ', color: 'rose' }))
    expect(out).toEqual({ tag: { ...tag, name: 'ПДВ', color: 'rose' }, recalc: false })
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tagKeys/name_vat`)).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tagKeys/name_пдв`)).toEqual({ tagId: tag.id })
    await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'vat' }))
    expect(await auditActions(db, ORG)).toEqual(['tag.create', 'tag.create', 'tag.update'])
  })

  it('a rename that only changes the case keeps the key', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'vat' }))
    await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ name: 'VAT' }))
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tagKeys/name_vat`)).toEqual({ tagId: tag.id })
    expect((await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${tag.id}`))?.name).toBe('VAT')
  })

  it('refuses a rename to the name of another tag', async () => {
    const vat = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'VAT' }))
    const atm = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'Bankomat' }))
    expect(await code(updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, atm.id, parseTagPatch({ name: 'vat' })))).toBe(`duplicate:name:${vat.id}`)
    expect((await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${atm.id}`))?.name).toBe('Bankomat')
  })

  it('archiving keeps the flag and needs no recalculation, even for a used tag', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ', excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
    await tagged(tag.id)
    expect(await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ archived: true }))).toEqual({ tag: { ...tag, archived: true }, recalc: false })
  })

  it('a change of the flag of a tag without operations needs no recalculation', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ' }))
    const out = await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
    expect(out).toEqual({ tag: { ...tag, excludeFromAnalytics: true }, recalc: false })
  })

  it('a change of the flag of a used tag sets recalcPending in the same write and asks for the recalculation', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ' }))
    await tagged(tag.id)
    const out = await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
    expect(out).toEqual({ tag: { ...tag, excludeFromAnalytics: true, recalcPending: true }, recalc: true })
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${tag.id}`)).toMatchObject({ excludeFromAnalytics: true, recalcPending: true, orgId: ORG })
  })

  it('an unknown tag is not_found', async () => {
    expect(await code(updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, 'ghost', parseTagPatch({ archived: true })))).toBe('not_found::')
  })
})

describe('listTags and tagImpact', () => {
  it('lists every tag, the archived ones too, by name', async () => {
    await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'Żywność' }))
    const old = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'Archiwum' }))
    await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, old.id, parseTagPatch({ archived: true }))
    await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'bank' }))
    expect((await listTags(db, ORG)).map((t) => [t.name, t.archived])).toEqual([
      ['Archiwum', true],
      ['bank', false],
      ['Żywność', false],
    ])
  })

  it('counts the operations that carry the tag; an unknown tag is not_found', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ' }))
    await tagged(tag.id)
    await tagged(tag.id)
    expect(await tagImpact(db, ORG, tag.id)).toBe(2)
    expect(await code(tagImpact(db, ORG, 'ghost'))).toBe('not_found::')
  })
})
```

Створити `lib/server/tags/write.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { Timestamp, type Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { parseTag, tagToDoc } from '@/lib/domain/tags/doc'
import { TagError } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import type { TagInput, TagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { tagNameKey } from '@/lib/domain/tags/keys'
import { MAX_TAGS, type Tag, type UpdateTagResult } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { auditData } from '@/lib/server/ledger/audit'
import { auditCol, orgRef, tagKeysCol, tagsCol, transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'

/** What a tag write needs: Admin Firestore and the clock (the ledger deps have both). */
export interface TagDeps {
  db: Firestore
  now: () => Date
  /** Overrides the per-company limit (tests). */
  maxCount?: number
}

function duplicate(existing: { get: (field: string) => unknown }): TagError {
  return new TagError('duplicate', { field: 'name', existingId: existing.get('tagId') as string })
}

/** A new tag. The limit, the name key, the tag and the audit entry are one transaction. */
export async function createTag(deps: TagDeps, orgId: string, uid: string, input: TagInput): Promise<Tag> {
  const ref = tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc()
  const keyRef = tagKeysCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(tagNameKey(input.name))
  const limit = deps.maxCount ?? MAX_TAGS
  return deps.db.runTransaction(async (t) => {
    // All reads first.
    const org = await t.get(orgRef(deps.db, orgId))
    const count = (await t.get(tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).count())).data().count
    const taken = await t.get(keyRef)

    if (!org.exists) throw new TagError('not_found')
    if (taken.exists) throw duplicate(taken)
    if (count >= limit) throw new TagError('limit_reached')

    const tag: Tag = { id: ref.id, name: input.name, color: input.color, excludeFromAnalytics: input.excludeFromAnalytics, archived: false, recalcPending: false }
    const ts = Timestamp.fromDate(deps.now())
    t.create(ref, { ...tagToDoc(tag), orgId, createdAt: ts, createdBy: uid, updatedAt: ts })
    t.create(keyRef, { tagId: ref.id })
    t.create(auditCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(), auditData(deps, 'tag.create', uid, ref.id, null, tagToDoc(tag)))
    return tag
  })
}

/**
 * Changes what the patch gives. A rename moves the name key. A change of `excludeFromAnalytics` of a tag that some
 * operation carries sets `recalcPending` in the same transaction and answers `recalc: true`: the browser then runs the
 * recalculation steps. Archiving never touches the flag and needs no recalculation.
 */
export async function updateTag(deps: TagDeps, orgId: string, uid: string, id: string, patch: TagPatch): Promise<UpdateTagResult> {
  const ref = tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(id)
  const keysCol = tagKeysCol(deps.db, orgId)
  return deps.db.runTransaction(async (t) => {
    const snap = await t.get(ref)
    const prior = snap.exists ? parseTag(snap.id, snap.data()) : null
    if (!prior) throw new TagError('not_found')

    const after: Tag = {
      ...prior,
      name: patch.name ?? prior.name,
      color: patch.color ?? prior.color,
      excludeFromAnalytics: patch.excludeFromAnalytics ?? prior.excludeFromAnalytics,
      archived: patch.archived ?? prior.archived,
    }
    const oldKey = tagNameKey(prior.name)
    const newKey = tagNameKey(after.name)
    const rekey = oldKey !== newKey
    const flagChanged = after.excludeFromAnalytics !== prior.excludeFromAnalytics
    // All reads first.
    const taken = rekey ? await t.get(keysCol.doc(newKey)) : null
    const leaving = rekey ? await t.get(keysCol.doc(oldKey)) : null
    const used = flagChanged ? await t.get(transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId).where('tags', 'array-contains', id).select().limit(1)) : null

    if (taken !== null && taken.exists && taken.get('tagId') !== id) throw duplicate(taken)
    const recalc = used !== null && !used.empty
    const stored: Tag = { ...after, recalcPending: recalc ? true : prior.recalcPending }

    // `update` keeps orgId, createdAt and createdBy.
    t.update(ref, { ...tagToDoc(stored), updatedAt: Timestamp.fromDate(deps.now()) })
    if (taken !== null) {
      if (leaving !== null && leaving.exists && leaving.get('tagId') === id) t.delete(keysCol.doc(oldKey))
      if (!taken.exists) t.create(keysCol.doc(newKey), { tagId: id })
    }
    t.create(auditCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(), auditData(deps, 'tag.update', uid, id, tagToDoc(prior), tagToDoc(stored)))
    return { tag: stored, recalc }
  })
}
```

Створити `lib/server/tags/read.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import type { Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { parseTag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/doc'
import { TagError } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import { sortTags } from '@/lib/domain/tags/search'
import type { Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { tagsCol, transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'

/** `GET /tags`: every readable tag of the company (at most 100), the archived ones too, by name. */
export async function listTags(db: Firestore, orgId: string): Promise<Tag[]> {
  const snap = await tagsCol(db, orgId).get()
  return sortTags(snap.docs.map((d) => parseTag(d.id, d.data())).filter((t): t is Tag => t !== null))
}

/** `GET /tags/{id}/impact`: how many operations carry the tag, by an aggregate `count()` (no document is read). */
export async function tagImpact(db: Firestore, orgId: string, tagId: string): Promise<number> {
  const snap = await tagsCol(db, orgId).doc(tagId).get()
  if (!snap.exists || parseTag(snap.id, snap.data()) === null) throw new TagError('not_found')
  return (await transactionsCol(db, orgId).where('tags', 'array-contains', tagId).count().get()).data().count
}
```

Створити `lib/server/tags/http.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { TagError, type TagErrorCode } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import { HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { toHttpError } from '@/lib/server/ledger/http'

/** The largest JSON body of a tag create or edit: a 50-character name and three small fields. */
export const TAG_BODY_MAX_BYTES = 4096

const STATUS: Record<TagErrorCode, number> = {
  invalid_input: 400,
  not_found: 404,
  duplicate: 409,
  limit_reached: 409,
}

/** A TagError becomes an HttpError (the code, plus `field` and `existingId` when set); others go to the ledger mapping. */
export function toTagHttpError(e: unknown): unknown {
  if (e instanceof TagError) {
    const extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    if (e.field !== undefined) extra.field = e.field
    if (e.existingId !== undefined) extra.existingId = e.existingId
    return new HttpError(STATUS[e.code], e.code, Object.keys(extra).length > 0 ? extra : undefined)
  }
  return toHttpError(e)
}
```

Створити `lib/server/tags/index.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import type { TagInput, TagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { ledgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger'
import { listTags, tagImpact } from './read'
import { createTag, updateTag } from './write'

/** The tags service bound to the production Firestore and clock. */
export const tags = {
  list: (orgId: string) => listTags(ledgerDeps().db, orgId),
  create: (orgId: string, uid: string, input: TagInput) => createTag(ledgerDeps(), orgId, uid, input),
  update: (orgId: string, uid: string, tagId: string, patch: TagPatch) => updateTag(ledgerDeps(), orgId, uid, tagId, patch),
  impact: (orgId: string, tagId: string) => tagImpact(ledgerDeps().db, orgId, tagId),
}
```

`firestore.rules`:

1. Рядок коментаря `//   organizations/{orgId}/{accounts|categories|transactions|rollups|audit|bankConnections|bankAccounts|bankSeen|ledger|counterparties|counterpartyKeys|rules}/{docId}` замінити на `//   organizations/{orgId}/{accounts|categories|transactions|rollups|audit|bankConnections|bankAccounts|bankSeen|ledger|counterparties|counterpartyKeys|rules|tags|tagKeys}/{docId}`.
2. Рядок `// Accounts, transactions, rollups, audit, ledger, counterparties, rules (S10) and the bank collections (bankConnections, bankAccounts,` замінити на `// Accounts, transactions, rollups, audit, ledger, counterparties, rules (S10), tags (S10t) and the bank collections (bankConnections, bankAccounts,`.
3. Рядок `// bankSeen: S09) are written by the server only (S05, S06, S09). counterpartyKeys (uniqueness keys), bankAccounts and bankSeen` замінити на `// bankSeen: S09) are written by the server only (S05, S06, S09). counterpartyKeys and tagKeys (uniqueness keys), bankAccounts and bankSeen`.
4. Рядок `        allow read: if (subcollection in ['accounts', 'categories', 'transactions', 'rollups', 'counterparties', 'rules']` замінити на `        allow read: if (subcollection in ['accounts', 'categories', 'transactions', 'rollups', 'counterparties', 'rules', 'tags']`.

`tagKeys` не згадується в жодному `allow`, тож закритий за замовчуванням.

```ts
/** A tag in the shape the server stores (lib/domain/tags/doc.ts) with its name key; `name` defaults to the id. */
export async function seedTag(
  db: Firestore,
  orgId: string,
  id: string,
  options: { name?: string; excludeFromAnalytics?: boolean; archived?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<void> {
  const name = options.name ?? id
  const doc = tagToDoc({ id, name, color: 'slate', excludeFromAnalytics: options.excludeFromAnalytics === true, archived: options.archived === true, recalcPending: false })
  await db.doc(`organizations/${orgId}/tags/${id}`).set({ ...doc, orgId })
  await db.doc(`organizations/${orgId}/tagKeys/${tagNameKey(name)}`).set({ tagId: id })
}
```

Створити `tests/integration/ledger-tags.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { LedgerError } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/errors'
import { parseTransactionInput, parseTransactionPatch } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import { rebuildLedger } from '@/lib/server/ledger/rebuild'
import { verifyLedger } from '@/lib/server/ledger/verify'
import { createTransaction, deleteTransaction, updateTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { ORG, UID, balanceOf, clearFirestore, makeDeps, readDoc, rollup, seedAccount, seedCategory, seedOrg, seedTag, testDb } from './helpers'

const db = testDb()
const deps = makeDeps(db)

/** An expense of 230 zł from the bank on 2026-10-20 with a category; `extra` replaces fields. */
const op = (extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
  parseTransactionInput({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'bank', amountMinor: 23_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-20', categoryId: 'k-ex', ...extra })
const txDoc = (id: string) => readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/transactions/${id}`)

async function code(p: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> {
  try {
    await p
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof LedgerError ? `${e.code}:${e.field}` : `other:${String(e)}`
  }
  return 'none'
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  await clearFirestore()
  await seedOrg(db)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'bank', 'PLN', 100_000)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'cash', 'PLN', 0)
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-ex', 'expense')
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-in', 'income')
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-fuel', 'expense')
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'vat', { name: 'ПДВ', excludeFromAnalytics: true })
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'atm', { name: 'Bankomat', excludeFromAnalytics: true })
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'q4', { name: 'Q4' })
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'old', { name: 'Stary', archived: true })
})

describe('tags on an operation', () => {
  it('an excluding tag sends the operation to the x_ rollups; the balance moves exactly as without the tag', async () => {
    const tagged = await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['vat'] }))
    expect(tagged.transaction.excluded).toBe(true)
    expect(await txDoc(tagged.transaction.id)).toMatchObject({ tags: ['vat'], excluded: true })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ outMinor: 23_000, outBase: 23_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cashcat_2026-10')).toEqual({ 'categories.k-ex.expenseBase': 23_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_pl_2026-10')).toEqual({ 'categories.k-ex.expenseBase': 23_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({})
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'pl_2026-10')).toEqual({})
    const afterTagged = await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')

    // The same operation without the tag: the balance moves by the same amount, the analytics rollups get it.
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op())
    expect(afterTagged).toBe(100_000 - 23_000)
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(100_000 - 2 * 23_000)
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ outMinor: 23_000, outBase: 23_000 })
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })

  it('a tag without the flag changes nothing in the rollups', async () => {
    const { transaction } = await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['q4'] }))
    expect(transaction.excluded).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await txDoc(transaction.id))?.excluded).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ outMinor: 23_000, outBase: 23_000 })
  })

  it('an unknown tag and an archived tag are refused and nothing is written', async () => {
    expect(await code(createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['ghost'] })))).toBe('tag_not_found:tags')
    expect(await code(createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['q4', 'old'] })))).toBe('tag_archived:tags')
    expect((await db.collection(`organizations/${ORG}/transactions`).get()).size).toBe(0)
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(100_000)
  })

  it('an edit that keeps a tag archived since, or takes it off, is allowed; giving it anew is not', async () => {
    const { transaction } = await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['q4'] }))
    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tags/q4`).update({ archived: true })
    await updateTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, transaction.id, parseTransactionPatch({ description: 'still fine' }))
    expect(await txDoc(transaction.id)).toMatchObject({ tags: ['q4'], description: 'still fine' })
    await updateTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, transaction.id, parseTransactionPatch({ tags: [] }))
    expect((await txDoc(transaction.id))?.tags).toEqual([])
    expect(await code(updateTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, transaction.id, parseTransactionPatch({ tags: ['q4'] })))).toBe('tag_archived:tags')
  })

  it('adding and taking off the excluding tag moves the rollups between the twins; the balance never moves', async () => {
    const { transaction } = await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op())
    await updateTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, transaction.id, parseTransactionPatch({ tags: ['vat'] }))
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(77_000)
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({})
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ outMinor: 23_000, outBase: 23_000 })

    await updateTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, transaction.id, parseTransactionPatch({ tags: [] }))
    expect((await txDoc(transaction.id))?.excluded).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({})
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ outMinor: 23_000, outBase: 23_000 })
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(77_000)

    await deleteTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, transaction.id)
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(100_000)
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })

  it('a new operation takes the flag of its tags as they are at the write', async () => {
    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tags/q4`).update({ excludeFromAnalytics: true })
    expect((await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['q4'] }))).transaction.excluded).toBe(true)
  })

  it('a request cannot set excluded itself', () => {
    expect(() => parseTransactionInput({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'bank', amountMinor: 1, paymentDate: '2026-10-20', excluded: true })).toThrow(LedgerError)
  })
})

describe('the owner scenarios (2026-10-29)', () => {
  it('a VAT pair with the tag: the analytics rollups show only the real sale, the bank balance is what the bank says', async () => {
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ type: 'income', categoryId: 'k-in', tags: ['vat'] }))
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['vat'] }))
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ type: 'income', categoryId: 'k-in', amountMinor: 100_000 }))
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ inMinor: 100_000, inBase: 100_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'pl_2026-10')).toEqual({ 'categories.k-in.incomeBase': 100_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cashcat_2026-10')).toEqual({ 'categories.k-in.incomeBase': 100_000 })
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(100_000 + 23_000 - 23_000 + 100_000)
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })

  it('the cash machine: −1000 from the bank and +1000 into the cash box with the tag leave the P&L alone; the real spending from the cash box counts', async () => {
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ amountMinor: 100_000, categoryId: undefined, tags: ['atm'] }))
    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ type: 'income', accountId: 'cash', amountMinor: 100_000, categoryId: undefined, tags: ['atm'] }))
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'pl_2026-10')).toEqual({})
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cashcat_2026-10')).toEqual({})
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(0)
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'cash')).toBe(100_000)

    await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ accountId: 'cash', amountMinor: 100_000, categoryId: 'k-fuel', description: 'benzyna' }))
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'pl_2026-10')).toEqual({ 'categories.k-fuel.expenseBase': 100_000 })
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'cash')).toBe(0)
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe('verify and rebuild', () => {
  it('verify reports an operation whose stored flag disagrees with its tags; rebuild sets the flag and the x_ rollups', async () => {
    const { transaction } = await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['q4'] }))
    // The flag changed behind the ledger's back (as if the recalculation had not run yet).
    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tags/q4`).update({ excludeFromAnalytics: true })
    const report = await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)
    expect(report.ok).toBe(false)
    expect(report.excludedDiffs).toEqual([{ txId: transaction.id, stored: false, expected: true }])
    expect(report.balanceDiffs).toEqual([])

    const rebuilt = await rebuildLedger(deps, ORG, UID, { apply: true })
    expect(rebuilt.applied).toBe(true)
    expect((await txDoc(transaction.id))?.excluded).toBe(true)
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({ outMinor: 23_000, outBase: 23_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-20')).toEqual({})
    expect(await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, excludedDiffs: [], rollupDiffs: [], balanceDiffs: [] })
  })

  it('a tag that no longer exists does not exclude; rebuild clears a flag it does not justify', async () => {
    const { transaction } = await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, op({ tags: ['vat'] }))
    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tags/vat`).delete()
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).excludedDiffs).toEqual([{ txId: transaction.id, stored: true, expected: false }])
    await rebuildLedger(deps, ORG, UID, { apply: true })
    expect((await txDoc(transaction.id))?.excluded).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

3. Замінити всю функцію `commit` (від `/** One Firestore transaction: operation + balances + rollup increments + audit. All reads come first. */` до її закривальної `}` перед `function isRecord`) на:

```ts
/** One Firestore transaction: operation + balances + rollup increments + audit. All reads come first. */
async function commit(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, uid: string, job: Job): Promise<WriteResult> {
  const { db } = deps
  const txRef = transactionsCol(db, orgId).doc(job.txId)

  return db.runTransaction(async (t) => {
    await assertUnlocked(t, deps, orgId)
    const orgSnap = await t.get(orgRef(db, orgId))
    const currentSnap = await t.get(txRef)

    const involved = new Set<string>([
      ...(job.prior ? accountIdsOf(job.prior.tx) : []),
      ...(job.next ? accountIdsOf(job.next.tx) : []),
    ])
    const accountSnaps = new Map<string, DocumentSnapshot>()
    for (const id of involved) accountSnaps.set(id, await t.get(accountsCol(db, orgId).doc(id)))
    const categoryId = job.next?.tx.categoryId
    const categorySnap = categoryId === undefined ? null : await t.get(categoriesCol(db, orgId).doc(categoryId))
    // A counterparty is checked only when this write gives the operation a new one: an edit of something else
    // must not fail because the counterparty was archived after the operation was made.
    const counterpartyId = job.next?.tx.counterpartyId
    const counterpartyIsNew = counterpartyId !== undefined && counterpartyId !== job.prior?.tx.counterpartyId
    const counterpartySnap = counterpartyId !== undefined && counterpartyIsNew ? await t.get(counterpartiesCol(db, orgId).doc(counterpartyId)) : null
    // Tags (S10t): every tag of the new state is read, so `excluded` follows the flags as they are inside this
    // transaction. Only a tag this write newly gives the operation is checked: an edit of something else, or taking an
    // archived tag off, never fails because of a tag.
    const nextTags = job.next?.tx.tags ?? []
    const tagSnaps: DocumentSnapshot[] = []
    for (const id of nextTags) tagSnaps.push(await t.get(tagsCol(db, orgId).doc(id)))

    // ---- checks (nothing is written yet) ----
    if (!orgSnap.exists) throw new LedgerError('not_found', 'org')
    const baseCurrency = orgSnap.get('currency') as string
    if (job.next && job.next.baseCurrency !== baseCurrency) throw new LedgerError('conflict')

    if (job.prior === null) {
      if (currentSnap.exists) {
        const existing = parseTransaction(currentSnap.id, currentSnap.data())
        if (!existing) throw new LedgerError('conflict')
        return { transaction: existing, replayed: true }
      }
    } else {
      if (!currentSnap.exists) throw new LedgerError('not_found')
      if (!currentSnap.updateTime || !currentSnap.updateTime.isEqual(job.prior.version)) throw new LedgerError('conflict')
    }

    for (const [id, snap] of accountSnaps) {
      const account = snap.exists ? parseAccount(snap.id, snap.data()) : null
      if (!account) throw new LedgerError('conflict')
      const assumed = job.next && Object.hasOwn(job.next.currencies, id) ? job.next.currencies[id] : undefined
      if (assumed !== undefined && assumed !== account.currency) throw new LedgerError('conflict')
    }
    if (categorySnap !== null && job.next) {
      if (!categorySnap.exists || categorySnap.get('type') !== job.next.tx.type) throw new LedgerError('category_not_found', 'categoryId')
    }
    if (counterpartySnap !== null) {
      if (!counterpartySnap.exists) throw new LedgerError('counterparty_not_found', 'counterpartyId')
      if (counterpartySnap.get('archived') === true) throw new LedgerError('counterparty_archived', 'counterpartyId')
    }
    const priorTags = job.prior?.tx.tags ?? []
    tagSnaps.forEach((snap, i) => {
      if (priorTags.includes(nextTags[i])) return
      if (!snap.exists) throw new LedgerError('tag_not_found', 'tags')
      if (snap.get('archived') === true) throw new LedgerError('tag_archived', 'tags')
    })

    // ---- writes ----
    const ts = Timestamp.fromDate(deps.now())
    // `excluded` is the ledger's own field: decided here from the tags read above, never taken from a request.
    const next = job.next ? withExcluded(job.next.tx, excludedFromTags(nextTags, tagFlagsOf(tagSnaps))) : null
    const change = changeSet(job.prior?.tx ?? null, next, baseCurrency)

    for (const [id, delta] of Object.entries(change.balanceDeltas)) {
      t.update(accountsCol(db, orgId).doc(id), { balanceMinor: FieldValue.increment(delta), updatedAt: ts })
    }
    for (const [id, flat] of change.rollupDeltas) {
      t.set(rollupsCol(db, orgId).doc(id), nest(flat, (n) => FieldValue.increment(n)), { merge: true })
    }

    if (next) {
      const old = currentSnap.data() ?? {}
      t.set(txRef, {
        ...transactionToDoc(next),
        // Provenance of a migrated operation (S05 migration) outlives edits.
        ...(job.prior && old.migratedFrom !== undefined ? { migratedFrom: old.migratedFrom } : {}),
        ...(job.prior && old.legacy !== undefined ? { legacy: old.legacy } : {}),
        orgId,
        createdAt: job.prior ? (old.createdAt ?? ts) : ts,
        createdBy: job.prior ? (old.createdBy ?? uid) : uid,
        updatedAt: ts,
        updatedBy: uid,
      })
    } else {
      t.delete(txRef)
    }

    t.create(
      auditCol(db, orgId).doc(),
      auditData(
        deps,
        job.action,
        uid,
        job.txId,
        job.prior ? transactionToDoc(job.prior.tx) : null,
        next ? transactionToDoc(next) : null,
        job.via === undefined ? undefined : { via: job.via },
      ),
    )

    return { transaction: next ?? (job.prior as NonNullable<typeof job.prior>).tx, replayed: false }
  })
}
```

Замінити `lib/server/ledger/verify.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import { flatten, diffRollups, type RollupDiff, type RollupDocs } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/rollups'
import { parseAccount, parseTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/doc'
import { LedgerError } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/errors'
import { computeLedger, type ExpectedLedger } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/expected'
import type { LedgerAccount, LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { excludedFromTags, tagFlagsOf, withExcluded, type TagFlags } from '@/lib/domain/tags/exclude'
import type { LedgerDeps } from './deps'
import { accountsCol, orgRef, rollupsCol, tagsCol, transactionsCol } from './paths'

export const REPORT_LIMIT = 50

export interface LedgerReport {
  ok: boolean
  accounts: number
  transactions: number
  /** Operations that exist but cannot be counted (not schema 2, missing account or base amounts). */
  uncountable: string[]
  /** Accounts that are not schema 2 documents. */
  unreadableAccounts: string[]
  balanceDiffs: Array<{ accountId: string; stored: number; expected: number }>
  rollupDiffs: RollupDiff[]
  /** Operations whose stored `excluded` differs from what their tags say now (S10t); a tag that is gone does not exclude. */
  excludedDiffs: Array<{ txId: string; stored: boolean; expected: boolean }>
  /** True when a list above was cut at REPORT_LIMIT entries. */
  truncated: boolean
}

export interface LoadedLedger {
  baseCurrency: string
  accounts: LedgerAccount[]
  unreadableAccounts: string[]
  txs: LedgerTransaction[]
  unreadableTransactions: string[]
  storedRollups: RollupDocs
  /** The flags of the company's tags (S10t), by id. */
  tags: ReadonlyMap<string, TagFlags>
}

export async function loadLedger(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string): Promise<LoadedLedger> {
  const org = await orgRef(deps.db, orgId).get()
  if (!org.exists) throw new LedgerError('not_found', 'org')

  const accounts: LedgerAccount[] = []
  const unreadableAccounts: string[] = []
  for (const doc of (await accountsCol(deps.db, orgId).get()).docs) {
    const account = parseAccount(doc.id, doc.data())
    if (account) accounts.push(account)
    else unreadableAccounts.push(doc.id)
  }

  const txs: LedgerTransaction[] = []
  const unreadableTransactions: string[] = []
  for (const doc of (await transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId).get()).docs) {
    const tx = parseTransaction(doc.id, doc.data())
    if (tx) txs.push(tx)
    else unreadableTransactions.push(doc.id)
  }

  const storedRollups: RollupDocs = new Map()
  for (const doc of (await rollupsCol(deps.db, orgId).get()).docs) storedRollups.set(doc.id, flatten(doc.data()))

  const tags = tagFlagsOf((await tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).get()).docs)

  return { baseCurrency: org.get('currency') as string, accounts, unreadableAccounts, txs, unreadableTransactions, storedRollups, tags }
}

/** The operations with `excluded` as their tags say now: what the rollups must reflect (S10t). */
export function withTagFlags(data: LoadedLedger): LedgerTransaction[] {
  return data.txs.map((tx) => withExcluded(tx, excludedFromTags(tx.tags, data.tags)))
}

export function buildReport(data: LoadedLedger, expected: ExpectedLedger): LedgerReport {
  const balanceDiffs = data.accounts
    .filter((a) => a.balanceMinor !== expected.balances[a.id])
    .map((a) => ({ accountId: a.id, stored: a.balanceMinor, expected: expected.balances[a.id] }))
  const rollupDiffs = diffRollups(data.storedRollups, expected.rollups)
  const uncountable = [...data.unreadableTransactions, ...expected.invalid]
  const excludedDiffs = data.txs
    .map((tx) => ({ txId: tx.id, stored: tx.excluded === true, expected: excludedFromTags(tx.tags, data.tags) }))
    .filter((d) => d.stored !== d.expected)
  const truncated =
    balanceDiffs.length > REPORT_LIMIT || rollupDiffs.length > REPORT_LIMIT || uncountable.length > REPORT_LIMIT || excludedDiffs.length > REPORT_LIMIT
  return {
    ok:
      balanceDiffs.length === 0 &&
      rollupDiffs.length === 0 &&
      uncountable.length === 0 &&
      data.unreadableAccounts.length === 0 &&
      excludedDiffs.length === 0,
    accounts: data.accounts.length,
    transactions: data.txs.length + data.unreadableTransactions.length,
    uncountable: uncountable.slice(0, REPORT_LIMIT),
    unreadableAccounts: data.unreadableAccounts.slice(0, REPORT_LIMIT),
    balanceDiffs: balanceDiffs.slice(0, REPORT_LIMIT),
    rollupDiffs: rollupDiffs.slice(0, REPORT_LIMIT),
    excludedDiffs: excludedDiffs.slice(0, REPORT_LIMIT),
    truncated,
  }
}

/** Read-only: compares stored balances, rollups and `excluded` flags with what the operations and their tags imply. */
export async function verifyLedger(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string): Promise<LedgerReport> {
  const data = await loadLedger(deps, orgId)
  return buildReport(data, computeLedger(data.accounts, withTagFlags(data), data.baseCurrency))
}
```

Замінити `lib/server/ledger/rebuild.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import { FieldValue, Timestamp, type WriteBatch } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { computeLedger } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/expected'
import { nest } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/rollups'
import { excludedFromTags } from '@/lib/domain/tags/exclude'
import { auditData } from './audit'
import type { LedgerDeps } from './deps'
import { acquireLock, releaseLock, renewLock } from './lock'
import { accountsCol, auditCol, rollupsCol, transactionsCol } from './paths'
import { buildReport, loadLedger, verifyLedger, withTagFlags, type LedgerReport } from './verify'

const BATCH_SIZE = 400

export interface RebuildResult extends LedgerReport {
  applied: boolean
}

/**
 * Dry run (`apply: false`) = verifyLedger. With `apply: true`: takes the ledger lock (writes answer
 * ledger_busy meanwhile), recomputes balances and rollups from the operations (their `excluded` taken from the tags as
 * they are now, S10t), overwrites what differs, sets the stored `excluded` flags again, deletes stale rollup documents
 * and writes an audit entry. The returned report describes what was found.
 */
export async function rebuildLedger(
  deps: LedgerDeps,
  orgId: string,
  uid: string,
  opts: { apply: boolean; lock?: string },
): Promise<RebuildResult> {
  if (!opts.apply) return { ...(await verifyLedger(deps, orgId)), applied: false }

  // A caller that already holds the lock (the migration) passes its token and keeps ownership of the release.
  const token = opts.lock ?? (await acquireLock(deps, orgId, uid))
  try {
    const data = await loadLedger(deps, orgId)
    const expected = computeLedger(data.accounts, withTagFlags(data), data.baseCurrency)
    const report = buildReport(data, expected)
    const ts = Timestamp.fromDate(deps.now())

    const ops: Array<(batch: WriteBatch) => void> = []
    for (const account of data.accounts) {
      if (account.balanceMinor !== expected.balances[account.id]) {
        ops.push((b) => b.update(accountsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(account.id), { balanceMinor: expected.balances[account.id], updatedAt: ts }))
      }
    }
    for (const id of data.storedRollups.keys()) {
      if (!expected.rollups.has(id)) ops.push((b) => b.delete(rollupsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(id)))
    }
    for (const [id, flat] of expected.rollups) {
      ops.push((b) => b.set(rollupsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(id), nest(flat)))
    }
    // S10t: the stored flag follows the tags again (the rollups above already count it so).
    for (const tx of data.txs) {
      const want = excludedFromTags(tx.tags, data.tags)
      if (want !== (tx.excluded === true)) {
        ops.push((b) => b.update(transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(tx.id), { excluded: want ? true : FieldValue.delete() }))
      }
    }
    ops.push((b) =>
      b.create(
        auditCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(),
        auditData(deps, 'ledger.rebuild', uid, orgId, null, {
          balanceDiffs: report.balanceDiffs.length,
          rollupDiffs: report.rollupDiffs.length,
          uncountable: report.uncountable.length,
          excludedDiffs: report.excludedDiffs.length,
        }),
      ),
    )

    for (let i = 0; i < ops.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) {
      // The lease is 5 minutes and nobody else renews it: prove we still own it before every batch, and stop when we do not.
      await renewLock(deps, orgId, token)
      const batch = deps.db.batch()
      for (const op of ops.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE)) op(batch)
      await batch.commit()
    }
    return { ...report, applied: true }
  } finally {
    if (opts.lock === undefined) await releaseLock(deps, orgId, token)
  }
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { LedgerError } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/errors'
import { parseTransactionInput } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import { TagError } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import { parseTagInput, parseTagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import type { RecalcStepResult, Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { acquireLock, releaseLock } from '@/lib/server/ledger/lock'
import { rebuildLedger } from '@/lib/server/ledger/rebuild'
import { verifyLedger } from '@/lib/server/ledger/verify'
import { createTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { recalcStep } from '@/lib/server/tags/recalc'
import { createTag, updateTag } from '@/lib/server/tags/write'
import { ORG, UID, balanceOf, clearFirestore, makeDeps, readDoc, rollup, seedAccount, seedCategory, seedOrg, testDb } from './helpers'

const deps = makeDeps(db)

const expense = (paymentDate: string, tags: string[] = []) =>
  createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, parseTransactionInput({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'bank', amountMinor: 1_000, paymentDate, categoryId: 'k-ex', tags }))

/** Every step until done, `size` operations a step. */
async function runAll(tag: Tag, size: number): Promise<RecalcStepResult[]> {
  const steps: RecalcStepResult[] = []
  let cursor: string | null = null
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
    const step = await recalcStep(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, cursor, size)
    steps.push(step)
    if (step.done) return steps
    cursor = step.cursor
  }
  throw new Error('the recalculation never ends')
}

async function code(p: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> {
  try {
    await p
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof LedgerError || e instanceof TagError) return e.code
    return `other:${String(e)}`
  }
  return 'none'
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  await clearFirestore()
  await seedOrg(db)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'bank', 'PLN', 100_000)
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-ex', 'expense')
})

describe('recalcStep', () => {
  it('a flag change recalculates the past in steps; afterwards verifyLedger is clean and rebuild finds nothing to change', async () => {
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ' }))
    for (let day = 10; day < 15; day++) await expense(`2026-10-${day}`, [tag.id])

    const { recalc } = await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
    expect(recalc).toBe(true)
    // Until the steps run, the stored flags are the old ones: verify sees the drift.
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).excludedDiffs).toHaveLength(5)

    const steps = await runAll(tag, 2)
    expect(steps.map((s) => [s.scanned, s.changed, s.done])).toEqual([
      [2, 2, false],
      [2, 2, false],
      [1, 1, true],
    ])
    expect(steps[2].cursor).toBeNull()
    expect((await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${tag.id}`))?.recalcPending).toBe(false)
    expect(await balanceOf(db, ORG, 'bank')).toBe(100_000 - 6 * 1_000)
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cash_bank_2026-10-10')).toEqual({ outMinor: 1_000, outBase: 1_000 })
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'cash_bank_2026-10-10')).toEqual({})
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'pl_2026-10')).toEqual({ 'categories.k-ex.expenseBase': 1_000 })

    expect(await rebuildLedger(deps, ORG, UID, { apply: true })).toMatchObject({ rollupDiffs: [], balanceDiffs: [], excludedDiffs: [] })
  })

    expect(await code(recalcStep(deps, ORG, UID, 'ghost', null))).toBe('not_found')
    const tag = await createTag(deps, ORG, UID, parseTagInput({ name: 'ПДВ' }))
    await expense('2026-10-10', [tag.id])
    await updateTag(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, parseTagPatch({ excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
    const token = await acquireLock(deps, ORG, UID)
    expect(await code(recalcStep(deps, ORG, UID, tag.id, null))).toBe('ledger_busy')
    await releaseLock(deps, ORG, token)
    expect((await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${tag.id}`))?.recalcPending).toBe(true)
    await runAll(tag, 100)
    expect((await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/tags/${tag.id}`))?.recalcPending).toBe(false)
  })
})
```
