`lib/server/ledger/write.ts`: у кінець файлу (після `deleteTransaction`) додати:

```ts
/**
 * Brings the stored `excluded` of one operation in line with its tags as they are now (S10t: a tag's flag changed after
 * the operation was written). One Firestore transaction through `changeSet`: only the rollups move (the balances never
 * depend on the flag), the operation keeps everything else, and the audit entry says `via: 'tag'`. False, writing
 * nothing, when the operation is gone, is not a ledger v2 document or already agrees.
 */
export async function refreshExcluded(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, uid: string, txId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const { db } = deps
  const txRef = transactionsCol(db, orgId).doc(txId)
  return db.runTransaction(async (t) => {
    await assertUnlocked(t, deps, orgId)
    const orgSnap = await t.get(orgRef(db, orgId))
    const snap = await t.get(txRef)
    const prior = snap.exists ? parseTransaction(snap.id, snap.data()) : null
    if (!orgSnap.exists || prior === null) return false
    const tagSnaps: DocumentSnapshot[] = []
    for (const id of prior.tags) tagSnaps.push(await t.get(tagsCol(db, orgId).doc(id)))
    const excluded = excludedFromTags(prior.tags, tagFlagsOf(tagSnaps))
    if (excluded === (prior.excluded === true)) return false

    const next = withExcluded(prior, excluded)
    const change = changeSet(prior, next, orgSnap.get('currency') as string)
    const ts = Timestamp.fromDate(deps.now())
    for (const [id, delta] of Object.entries(change.balanceDeltas)) {
      t.update(accountsCol(db, orgId).doc(id), { balanceMinor: FieldValue.increment(delta), updatedAt: ts })
    }
    for (const [id, flat] of change.rollupDeltas) {
      t.set(rollupsCol(db, orgId).doc(id), nest(flat, (n) => FieldValue.increment(n)), { merge: true })
    }
    t.update(txRef, { excluded: excluded ? true : FieldValue.delete(), updatedAt: ts, updatedBy: uid })
    t.create(auditCol(db, orgId).doc(), auditData(deps, 'tx.update', uid, txId, transactionToDoc(prior), transactionToDoc(next), { via: 'tag' }))
    return true
  })
}
```

Створити `lib/server/tags/recalc.ts`:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { FieldPath, Timestamp } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { parseTag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/doc'
import { TagError } from '@/lib/domain/tags/errors'
import { RECALC_PER_STEP, type RecalcStepResult } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { tagsCol, transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'
import { refreshExcluded } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'

/**
 * One step of the recalculation after a tag's flag changed (S10t): the next `limit` operations that carry the tag, in
 * the order of their ids (`array-contains` over the automatic single-field index), each brought in line with its tags
 * in its own Firestore transaction (`refreshExcluded`). Safe to repeat: an operation that already agrees is left alone.
 * The step that reads the last operations clears `recalcPending`. A ledger refusal (`ledger_busy` during a rebuild)
 * ends the step with that error; the next call starts again from the same cursor.
 */
export async function recalcStep(
  deps: LedgerDeps,
  orgId: string,
  uid: string,
  tagId: string,
  cursor: string | null,
  limit: number = RECALC_PER_STEP,
): Promise<RecalcStepResult> {
  const tagRef = tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(tagId)
  const tagSnap = await tagRef.get()
  if (!tagSnap.exists || parseTag(tagSnap.id, tagSnap.data()) === null) throw new TagError('not_found')

  let q = transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId).where('tags', 'array-contains', tagId).orderBy(FieldPath.documentId()).select()
  if (cursor !== null) q = q.startAfter(cursor)
  const snap = await q.limit(limit).get()
  let changed = 0
  for (const d of snap.docs) if (await refreshExcluded(deps, orgId, uid, d.id)) changed++

  const done = snap.size < limit
  if (done) {
    await deps.db.runTransaction(async (t) => {
      const current = await t.get(tagRef)
      if (current.exists && current.get('recalcPending') === true) t.update(tagRef, { recalcPending: false, updatedAt: Timestamp.fromDate(deps.now()) })
    })
  }
  return { scanned: snap.size, changed, done, cursor: done || snap.size === 0 ? null : snap.docs[snap.size - 1].id }
}
```

Створити `app/api/orgs/[orgId]/tags/route.ts`:

```ts
import { parseTagInput } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { requireOrgRole, requireUser } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { errorResponse } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { readJsonBody } from '@/lib/server/request-body'
import { tags } from '@/lib/server/tags'
import { TAG_BODY_MAX_BYTES, toTagHttpError } from '@/lib/server/tags/http'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ orgId: string }> }

// GET /api/orgs/{orgId}/tags  (owner, accountant, viewer) -> { tags } by name, the archived ones too
export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant', 'viewer'])
    return Response.json({ tags: await tags.list(orgId) }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toTagHttpError(e))
  }
}

// POST /api/orgs/{orgId}/tags  (owner, accountant) { name, color?, excludeFromAnalytics? } -> 201 { tag }
export async function POST(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant'])
    const input = parseTagInput(await readJsonBody(req, TAG_BODY_MAX_BYTES))
    const tag = await tags.create(orgId, uid, input)
    return Response.json({ tag }, { status: 201, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toTagHttpError(e))
  }
}
```

Створити `app/api/orgs/[orgId]/tags/[tagId]/route.ts`:

```ts
import { isId } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { parseTagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { requireOrgRole, requireUser } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { errorResponse, HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { readJsonBody } from '@/lib/server/request-body'
import { tags } from '@/lib/server/tags'
import { TAG_BODY_MAX_BYTES, toTagHttpError } from '@/lib/server/tags/http'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ orgId: string; tagId: string }> }

// PATCH /api/orgs/{orgId}/tags/{tagId}  (owner, accountant) { name?, color?, excludeFromAnalytics?, archived? }
// -> { tag, recalc }: with recalc the browser runs POST .../recalculate until done
export async function PATCH(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId, tagId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant'])
    if (!isId(tagId)) throw new HttpError(404, 'not_found')
    const patch = parseTagPatch(await readJsonBody(req, TAG_BODY_MAX_BYTES))
    return Response.json(await tags.update(orgId, uid, tagId, patch), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toTagHttpError(e))
  }
}
```

Створити `app/api/orgs/[orgId]/tags/[tagId]/impact/route.ts`:

```ts
import { isId } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { requireOrgRole, requireUser } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { errorResponse, HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { tags } from '@/lib/server/tags'
import { toTagHttpError } from '@/lib/server/tags/http'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'

type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ orgId: string; tagId: string }> }

// GET /api/orgs/{orgId}/tags/{tagId}/impact  (owner, accountant) -> { operations }: what a change of the flag recalculates
export async function GET(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId, tagId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant'])
    if (!isId(tagId)) throw new HttpError(404, 'not_found')
    return Response.json({ operations: await tags.impact(orgId, tagId) }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toTagHttpError(e))
  }
}
```

Створити `app/api/orgs/[orgId]/tags/[tagId]/recalculate/route.ts`:

```ts
import { isId } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { parseRecalcRequest } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { requireOrgRole, requireUser } from '@/lib/server/auth'
import { errorResponse, HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { readJsonBody } from '@/lib/server/request-body'
import { tags } from '@/lib/server/tags'
import { toTagHttpError } from '@/lib/server/tags/http'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
// A step is up to 100 Firestore transactions, one per operation.
export const maxDuration = 60

const MAX_BODY_BYTES = 1024
type Ctx = { params: Promise<{ orgId: string; tagId: string }> }

// POST /api/orgs/{orgId}/tags/{tagId}/recalculate  (owner, accountant) { cursor? }
// One step -> { scanned, changed, done, cursor }. Call again with the cursor while done is false; repeating is safe.
export async function POST(req: Request, { params }: Ctx) {
  try {
    const { uid } = await requireUser(req)
    const { orgId, tagId } = await params
    await requireOrgRole(uid, orgId, ['owner', 'accountant'])
    if (!isId(tagId)) throw new HttpError(404, 'not_found')
    const { cursor } = parseRecalcRequest(await readJsonBody(req, MAX_BODY_BYTES))
    return Response.json(await tags.recalculate(orgId, uid, tagId, cursor), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } })
  } catch (e) {
    return errorResponse(toTagHttpError(e))
  }
}
```

```ts
})

async function rowsFor(patch: Partial<JournalFilters>) {

}

describe('the journal and tags (S10t)', () => {

      [ids.inc2, 100_000 + 10_000 - 2_300 - 1_000 + 5_000],
      [ids.inc1, 100_000 + 10_000],
    ])
  })
})
```

`lib/domain/journal/selection.ts`: замінити інтерфейс `BulkPatch` на:

```ts
/** What a bulk change may set. `null` clears the field. */
export interface BulkPatch {
  categoryId?: string | null
  counterpartyId?: string | null
  description?: string | null
  paymentDate?: string
  accrual?: Accrual
  /** Tags to add (S10t). A tag change never goes together with the fields above. */
  addTagIds?: string[]
  /** Tags to take off (S10t). */
  removeTagIds?: string[]
}

/** A bulk change of the tags (S10t): the server computes each operation's new list instead of setting a field. */
export function isTagPatch(patch: BulkPatch): boolean {
  return patch.addTagIds !== undefined || patch.removeTagIds !== undefined
}
```

Замінити `lib/domain/journal/bulk-input.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import { isDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { asRecord, bad, isId, onlyKeys } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { parseTransactionPatch } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import { tagIdsOf } from '@/lib/domain/tags/assign'
import { MAX_TX_TAGS } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import type { BulkPatch } from './selection'

/** One request carries at most this many operations; the browser sends a larger selection in several requests. */
export const MAX_BULK_IDS = 25

export type BulkOutcome = { id: string; ok: true } | { id: string; ok: false; code: string }

export type BulkRequest = { action: 'delete'; ids: string[] } | { action: 'update'; ids: string[]; patch: BulkPatch }

const PATCH_KEYS = ['categoryId', 'counterpartyId', 'description', 'paymentDate', 'accrual', 'addTagIds', 'removeTagIds'] as const
const TAG_KEYS: readonly string[] = ['addTagIds', 'removeTagIds']

function tagIds(v: unknown, field: string): string[] {
  const ids = tagIdsOf(v, MAX_TX_TAGS)
  if (ids === null || ids.length === 0) bad(field)
  return ids
}

/** A tag change (S10t) is a bulk change of its own: the new list is computed for each operation from the tags it has. */
function tagPatch(patch: Record<string, unknown>): BulkPatch {
  if (Object.keys(patch).some((k) => !TAG_KEYS.includes(k))) bad('patch')
  const out: BulkPatch = {}
  if (patch.addTagIds !== undefined) out.addTagIds = tagIds(patch.addTagIds, 'patch.addTagIds')
  if (patch.removeTagIds !== undefined) out.removeTagIds = tagIds(patch.removeTagIds, 'patch.removeTagIds')
  if ((out.addTagIds ?? []).some((id) => (out.removeTagIds ?? []).includes(id))) bad('patch.removeTagIds')
  return out
}

export function parseBulkRequest(body: unknown): BulkRequest {
  const r = asRecord(body)
  onlyKeys(r, ['action', 'ids', 'patch'])
  const ids = r.ids
  if (!Array.isArray(ids) || ids.length === 0 || ids.length > MAX_BULK_IDS) bad('ids')
  if (!ids.every((id) => isId(id)) || new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) bad('ids')

  if (r.action === 'delete') {
    if (r.patch !== undefined) bad('patch')
    return { action: 'delete', ids: ids as string[] }
  }
  if (r.action !== 'update') bad('action')

  const patch = asRecord(r.patch, 'patch')
  onlyKeys(patch, PATCH_KEYS, 'patch.')
  if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) bad('patch')
  if (Object.keys(patch).some((k) => TAG_KEYS.includes(k))) return { action: 'update', ids: ids as string[], patch: tagPatch(patch) }
  if (patch.paymentDate !== undefined && !isDay(patch.paymentDate)) bad('patch.paymentDate')
  // The field-level rules (types, nulls, accrual shape) are the ones the single-operation PATCH enforces.
  parseTransactionPatch(patch)
  return { action: 'update', ids: ids as string[], patch: patch as BulkPatch }
}
```

Замінити `lib/domain/journal/filter.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import { isDay } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/days'
import { isId } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/guards'
import { isMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import { TX_STATUSES, TX_TYPES, type LedgerTransaction, type TxStatus, type TxType } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'

export type DateBasis = 'payment' | 'accrual'

/** `tg=_none`: the operations without any tag (S10t). Tag ids are Firestore auto ids, so they are never `_none`. */
export const NO_TAG = '_none'

export interface JournalFilters {
  /** Inclusive days, compared with the payment date or with the accrual date or period (`basis`). */
  from?: string
  to?: string
  basis: DateBasis
  /** Free text: account name, counterparty name, comment. */
  q?: string
  /** Inclusive, in the minor units of the operation's own currency. */
  minMinor?: number
  maxMinor?: number
  counterpartyIds: string[]
  types: TxType[]
  status?: TxStatus
  accountId?: string
  categoryIds: string[]
  /** Any of these tags (S10t); `NO_TAG` stands for «без тегів». */
  tagIds: string[]
  /** Only the operations excluded from analytics (S10t). */
  excludedOnly: boolean
}

export const EMPTY_FILTERS: JournalFilters = { basis: 'payment', counterpartyIds: [], types: [], categoryIds: [], tagIds: [], excludedOnly: false }

const MAX_LIST = 30
const MAX_QUERY = 100

/** Lower case, no diacritics; `ł` has no decomposition, so it is mapped by hand. */
export function normalizeText(s: string): string {
  return s
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '')
    .replace(/ł/gi, 'l')
    .toLowerCase()
}

function ids(raw: string | null): string[] {
  if (!raw) return []
  return raw
    .split(',')
    .filter((s) => isId(s))
    .slice(0, MAX_LIST)
}

function minor(raw: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (raw === null || !/^\d+$/.test(raw)) return undefined
  const n = Number(raw)
  return isMinor(n) ? n : undefined
}

/** Lenient on purpose: a hand-edited link with a bad value just loses that value. */
export function parseFilters(p: URLSearchParams): JournalFilters {
  const f: JournalFilters = {
    ...EMPTY_FILTERS,
    counterpartyIds: ids(p.get('cp')),
    categoryIds: ids(p.get('cat')),
    tagIds: ids(p.get('tg')),
    excludedOnly: p.get('ex') === '1',
  }
  const from = p.get('from')
  const to = p.get('to')
  if (isDay(from)) f.from = from
  if (isDay(to)) f.to = to
  if (p.get('basis') === 'accrual') f.basis = 'accrual'
  const q = p.get('q')?.trim()
  if (q) f.q = q.slice(0, MAX_QUERY)
  const min = minor(p.get('min'))
  const max = minor(p.get('max'))
  if (min !== undefined) f.minMinor = min
  if (max !== undefined) f.maxMinor = max
  f.types = (p.get('types') ?? '')
    .split(',')
    .filter((t): t is TxType => (TX_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(t))
  const status = p.get('status')
  if (status !== null && (TX_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(status)) f.status = status as TxStatus
  const account = p.get('account')
  if (isId(account)) f.accountId = account
  return f
}

export function filtersToParams(f: JournalFilters): URLSearchParams {
  const p = new URLSearchParams()
  if (f.from) p.set('from', f.from)
  if (f.to) p.set('to', f.to)
  if (f.basis === 'accrual') p.set('basis', 'accrual')
  if (f.q) p.set('q', f.q)
  if (f.minMinor !== undefined) p.set('min', String(f.minMinor))
  if (f.maxMinor !== undefined) p.set('max', String(f.maxMinor))
  if (f.counterpartyIds.length) p.set('cp', f.counterpartyIds.join(','))
  if (f.types.length) p.set('types', f.types.join(','))
  if (f.status) p.set('status', f.status)
  if (f.accountId) p.set('account', f.accountId)
  if (f.categoryIds.length) p.set('cat', f.categoryIds.join(','))
  if (f.tagIds.length) p.set('tg', f.tagIds.join(','))
  if (f.excludedOnly) p.set('ex', '1')
  return p
}

/** True when something narrows the list; the date basis alone narrows nothing. */
export function hasActiveFilters(f: JournalFilters): boolean {
  return (
    f.from !== undefined ||
    f.to !== undefined ||
    !!f.q ||
    f.minMinor !== undefined ||
    f.maxMinor !== undefined ||
    f.counterpartyIds.length > 0 ||
    f.types.length > 0 ||
    f.status !== undefined ||
    f.accountId !== undefined ||
    f.categoryIds.length > 0 ||
    f.tagIds.length > 0 ||
    f.excludedOnly
  )
}

/** Names the text search looks in. */
export interface FilterContext {
  accountName: (id: string) => string | undefined
  counterpartyName: (id: string) => string | undefined
}

function accrualBounds(tx: LedgerTransaction): { from: string; to: string } {
  return tx.accrual.mode === 'date' ? { from: tx.accrual.date, to: tx.accrual.date } : { from: tx.accrual.from, to: tx.accrual.to }
}

/** The whole predicate, so the in-memory part of a page read and the tests use one definition. */
export function matchesFilters(tx: LedgerTransaction, f: JournalFilters, ctx: FilterContext): boolean {
  if (f.types.length && !f.types.includes(tx.type)) return false
  if (f.status && tx.status !== f.status) return false
  if (f.minMinor !== undefined && tx.amountMinor < f.minMinor) return false
  if (f.maxMinor !== undefined && tx.amountMinor > f.maxMinor) return false
  if (f.categoryIds.length && (tx.categoryId === undefined || !f.categoryIds.includes(tx.categoryId))) return false
  if (f.counterpartyIds.length && (tx.counterpartyId === undefined || !f.counterpartyIds.includes(tx.counterpartyId))) return false
  if (f.accountId && tx.accountId !== f.accountId && tx.toAccountId !== f.accountId) return false
  if (f.tagIds.length) {
    const untagged = tx.tags.length === 0 && f.tagIds.includes(NO_TAG)
    if (!untagged && !tx.tags.some((id) => f.tagIds.includes(id))) return false
  }
  if (f.excludedOnly && tx.excluded !== true) return false

  if (f.from !== undefined || f.to !== undefined) {
    const range = f.basis === 'payment' ? { from: tx.paymentDate, to: tx.paymentDate } : accrualBounds(tx)
    if (f.from !== undefined && range.to < f.from) return false
    if (f.to !== undefined && range.from > f.to) return false
  }

  if (f.q) {
    const hay = [
      ctx.accountName(tx.accountId),
      tx.toAccountId === undefined ? undefined : ctx.accountName(tx.toAccountId),
      tx.counterpartyId === undefined ? undefined : ctx.counterpartyName(tx.counterpartyId),
      tx.description,
    ]
      .filter((s): s is string => !!s)
      .map(normalizeText)
    const words = normalizeText(f.q).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean)
    if (!words.every((w) => hay.some((h) => h.includes(w)))) return false
  }
  return true
}
```

`lib/server/ledger/write.ts`: після рядка `import { excludedFromTags, tagFlagsOf, withExcluded } from '@/lib/domain/tags/exclude'` додати:

```ts
import { mergeTags, sameTags, type TagChange } from '@/lib/domain/tags/assign'
```

і після функції `deleteTransaction` (перед `refreshExcluded`) додати:

```ts
/**
 * Adds and takes off tags (the bulk change of the journal, S10t). The new list comes from the operation as it is stored
 * (`mergeTags`: takes off first, then adds while there are fewer than 10) and goes through `updateTransaction` with the
 * version read here, so a change made in between is a `conflict`, never lost. A list that stays the same writes nothing.
 */
export async function retagTransaction(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, uid: string, txId: string, change: TagChange): Promise<WriteResult> {
  const prior = await loadPrior(deps, orgId, txId)
  const tags = mergeTags(prior.tx.tags, change.add, change.remove)
  if (sameTags(tags, prior.tx.tags)) return { transaction: prior.tx, replayed: false }
  return updateTransaction(deps, orgId, uid, txId, { tags }, { expectVersion: prior.version })
}
```

Замінити `lib/server/journal/bulk.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import type { BulkOutcome, BulkRequest } from '@/lib/domain/journal/bulk-input'
import { isTagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/journal/selection'
import type { TransactionPatch } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import type { TagChange } from '@/lib/domain/tags/assign'
import { HttpError } from '@/lib/server/http-error'
import { toHttpError } from '@/lib/server/ledger/http'

/** The ledger calls a bulk change is made of (the production `ledger` object has them). */
export interface BulkLedger {
  updateTransaction(orgId: string, uid: string, txId: string, patch: TransactionPatch): Promise<unknown>
  deleteTransaction(orgId: string, uid: string, txId: string): Promise<unknown>
  /** Adds and takes off tags (S10t): the new list is computed from the operation as it is stored. */
  retagTransaction(orgId: string, uid: string, txId: string, change: TagChange): Promise<unknown>
}

/**
 * One operation at a time through the same validation and Firestore transaction as an edit in the form, so balances,
 * rollups and the audit trail are right by construction. A refusal (a ledger or rate error) is that row's outcome and the
 * rest go on; so is an unexpected error (outcome `unknown`, logged once): rethrowing would lose the outcomes of the rows
 * already applied, and the browser could neither report them nor offer their undo.
 */
export async function runBulk(ledger: BulkLedger, orgId: string, uid: string, req: BulkRequest): Promise<BulkOutcome[]> {
  const out: BulkOutcome[] = []
  for (const id of req.ids) {
    try {
      if (req.action === 'delete') await ledger.deleteTransaction(orgId, uid, id)
      else if (isTagPatch(req.patch)) await ledger.retagTransaction(orgId, uid, id, { add: req.patch.addTagIds ?? [], remove: req.patch.removeTagIds ?? [] })
      else await ledger.updateTransaction(orgId, uid, id, req.patch as TransactionPatch)
      out.push({ id, ok: true })
    } catch (e) {
      const mapped = toHttpError(e)
      if (mapped instanceof HttpError && (mapped.status < 500 || mapped.status === 503)) {
        // The short code is the error message (`new HttpError(status, code, extra?)`).
        out.push({ id, ok: false, code: mapped.message })
      } else {
        // No request body, tokens or patch in the log: the org, the operation and what went wrong.
        console.error('[journal.bulk] unexpected error', { orgId, txId: id, error: e instanceof Error ? `${e.name}: ${e.message}` : String(e) })
        out.push({ id, ok: false, code: 'unknown' })
      }
    }
  }
  return out
}
```

3. Замінити doc-коментар і всю функцію `netsBetween` на:

```ts
/**
 * Net movement per day for the days from `oldest` to `newest` that have a rollup. An operation excluded from analytics
 * (S10t) still moved the account: its day sits in the `x_` twin of the daily rollup, which is read and added as well.
 */
async function netsBetween(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, accountId: string, oldest: string, newest: string, stats: ReadStats): Promise<Map<string, number>> {
  const col = rollupsCol(deps.db, orgId)
  const out = new Map<string, number>()
  const idsOf = [(day: string) => cashDocId(accountId, day), (day: string) => excludedDocId(cashDocId(accountId, day))]
  await Promise.all(
    idsOf.map(async (idOf) => {
      const snap = await col
        .where(FieldPath.documentId(), '>=', col.doc(idOf(oldest)))
        .where(FieldPath.documentId(), '<=', col.doc(idOf(newest)))
        .get()
      stats.docsRead += snap.size
      const prefix = idOf('')
      for (const d of snap.docs) {
        const day = d.id.slice(prefix.length)
        out.set(day, (out.get(day) ?? 0) + netOf(d.data() as DayNet))
      }
    }),
  )
  return out
}
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { parseTransactionInput } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import { RuleError } from '@/lib/domain/rules/errors'
import type { Rule } from '@/lib/domain/rules/types'
import { createWithRules, syncStep } from '@/lib/server/banking/sync'
import { verifyLedger } from '@/lib/server/ledger/verify'
import { createTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { applyStep, checkStep, previewRule } from '@/lib/server/rules/apply'
import { listRules } from '@/lib/server/rules/read'
import { createRule, updateRule } from '@/lib/server/rules/write'
import { bankDeps, ebTx, fakeEb, seedBank } from './bank-helpers'
import { ORG, UID, clearFirestore, makeDeps, readDoc, rollup, seedAccount, seedCategory, seedCounterparty, seedOrg, seedTag, testDb } from './helpers'
import { ruleInput } from './rule-helpers'

const db = testDb()
const deps = makeDeps(db)
const txCol = () => db.collection(`organizations/${ORG}/transactions`)

const PAGE = {
  '': {
    transactions: [
      ebTx('r1', '50.00', 'DBIT', '2026-10-25', { creditor: { name: 'ORLEN STACJA 123' }, remittance_information: ['Płatność kartą'] }),
      ebTx('r2', '20.00', 'DBIT', '2026-10-26', { creditor: { name: 'Kawiarnia' }, remittance_information: ['Kawa'] }),
    ],
    continuationKey: null,
  },
}

async function byRef(ref: string): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
  const snap = await txCol().where('bank.entryReference', '==', ref).get()
  if (snap.empty) throw new Error(`no operation for ${ref}`)
  return snap.docs[0].data()
}

async function code(p: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> {
  try {
    await p
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof RuleError ? `${e.code}:${e.field}` : `other:${String(e)}`
  }
  return 'none'
}

async function runToEnd(deps: ReturnType<typeof bankDeps>): Promise<void> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) if ((await syncStep(deps, ORG, UID, 'ba1')).done) return
  throw new Error('sync did not finish')
}

const write = async (extra: Record<string, unknown>) =>
  (await createTransaction(deps, ORG, UID, parseTransactionInput({ type: 'expense', accountId: 'a1', amountMinor: 1_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-20', ...extra }))).transaction.id

beforeEach(async () => {
  await clearFirestore()
  await seedOrg(db)
  await seedAccount(db, ORG, 'a1', 'PLN', 0)
  await seedCategory(db, ORG, 'k-fuel', 'expense')
  await seedCounterparty(db, ORG, 'cp1')
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'vat', { excludeFromAnalytics: true })
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'q4')
  await seedTag(db, ORG, 'old', { archived: true })
})

describe('a rule that adds tags (S10t)', () => {
  it('a create refuses an unknown or archived tag; an edit checks only the tags it adds, so an unusable rule can still be switched off', async () => {
    expect(await code(createRule(deps, ORG, UID, ruleInput({ actions: { tagIds: ['ghost'] } })))).toBe('tag_not_found:actions.tagIds')
    expect(await code(createRule(deps, ORG, UID, ruleInput({ actions: { tagIds: ['old'] } })))).toBe('tag_archived:actions.tagIds')
    const rule = await createRule(deps, ORG, UID, ruleInput({ actions: { tagIds: ['q4'] } }))
    await db.doc(`organizations/${ORG}/tags/q4`).update({ archived: true })
    await updateRule(deps, ORG, UID, rule.id, { active: false })
    expect((await listRules(db, ORG))[0].unusable).toEqual(['tags'])
    expect(await code(updateRule(deps, ORG, UID, rule.id, { actions: { tagIds: ['q4', 'old'] } }))).toBe('tag_archived:actions.tagIds')
  })

  it('the bank import adds the tags next to the category; the excluding tag sends the operation to the x_ rollups', async () => {
    await createRule(deps, ORG, UID, ruleInput({ actions: { categoryId: 'k-fuel', tagIds: ['vat'] } }))
    await seedBank(db)
    const bank = bankDeps(db, fakeEb(PAGE))
    await runToEnd(bank)
    expect(await byRef('r1')).toMatchObject({ categoryId: 'k-fuel', tags: ['vat'], excluded: true })
    expect((await byRef('r2')).tags).toEqual([])
    expect(await rollup(db, ORG, 'x_cash_a1_2026-10-25')).toMatchObject({ outMinor: 5_000 })
    expect((await verifyLedger(bank.ledger, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
  })

  it('when the ledger refuses a tag the rules gave (archived after they were read), the operation is written without the rule', async () => {
    await seedBank(db)
    const bank = bankDeps(db, fakeEb(PAGE))
    const stale: Rule = {
      id: 'r-stale', name: 'Stale', type: 'expense', active: true, order: 0, createdAtMs: 0,
      conditions: [{ kind: 'text', op: 'contains', values: ['orlen'] }], actions: { categoryId: 'k-fuel', tagIds: ['old'] },
    }
    const input = parseTransactionInput({ clientId: 'bk_tagfallback1', type: 'expense', status: 'actual', accountId: 'a1', amountMinor: 5_000, paymentDate: '2026-10-25' }, { internal: true })
    await createWithRules(bank, ORG, UID, input, { accountRef: 'ba1', counterpartyName: 'ORLEN STACJA 123' }, 'PLN', [stale])
    const doc = await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/transactions/bk_tagfallback1`)
    expect(doc).toMatchObject({ source: 'bank', tags: [] })
    expect(doc?.categoryId).toBeUndefined()
    expect(doc?.autoRules).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it('«Застосувати до наявних» adds the tags to an operation that already has a category; the check counts them', async () => {
    const tagged = await write({ description: 'ORLEN', categoryId: 'k-fuel', tags: ['q4'] })
    await write({ description: 'Kawa' })
    await createRule(deps, ORG, UID, ruleInput({ actions: { tagIds: ['vat'] } }))
    expect(await checkStep(deps, ORG, null)).toMatchObject({ scanned: 2, matchOps: 1, categories: 0, counterparties: 0, tags: 1, done: true })
    expect(await applyStep(deps, ORG, UID, null)).toMatchObject({ changed: 1, failed: 0, done: true })
    expect(await readDoc(db, `organizations/${ORG}/transactions/${tagged}`)).toMatchObject({
      tags: ['q4', 'vat'],
      excluded: true,
      autoRules: [{ id: expect.any(String), name: 'Paliwo' }],
    })
    expect((await verifyLedger(deps, ORG)).ok).toBe(true)
    expect(await checkStep(deps, ORG, null)).toMatchObject({ matchOps: 0, tags: 0 })
  })

  it('«Перевірити» counts an operation with a category and a counterparty that lacks a tag of the rule', async () => {
    await write({ description: 'ORLEN', categoryId: 'k-fuel', counterpartyId: 'cp1' })
    expect(await previewRule(deps, ORG, ruleInput({ actions: { tagIds: ['vat'] } }))).toEqual({ matched: 1, scanned: 1, capped: false })
    expect(await previewRule(deps, ORG, ruleInput({ actions: { categoryId: 'k-fuel' } }))).toEqual({ matched: 0, scanned: 0, capped: false })
  })
})
```

```ts
function actions(v: unknown): RuleActions {
  const r = asRecord(v, 'actions')
  onlyKeys(r, ['categoryId', 'counterpartyId', 'tagIds'], 'actions.')
  const out: RuleActions = {}
  if (r.categoryId !== undefined) {
    if (!isId(r.categoryId)) bad('actions.categoryId')
    out.categoryId = r.categoryId
  }
  if (r.counterpartyId !== undefined) {
    if (!isId(r.counterpartyId)) bad('actions.counterpartyId')
    out.counterpartyId = r.counterpartyId
  }
  if (r.tagIds !== undefined) {
    const tagIds = tagIdsOf(r.tagIds, MAX_RULE_TAGS)
    if (tagIds === null || tagIds.length === 0) bad('actions.tagIds')
    out.tagIds = tagIds
  }
  if (out.categoryId === undefined && out.counterpartyId === undefined && out.tagIds === undefined) bad('actions')
  return out
}
```

і замінити функцію `parseActions` на:

```ts
function parseActions(v: unknown): RuleActions | null {
  if (!isRecord(v)) return null
  const out: RuleActions = {}
  for (const key of ['categoryId', 'counterpartyId'] as const) {
    const value = v[key]
    if (value === undefined) continue
    if (!isId(value)) return null
    out[key] = value
  }
  if (v.tagIds !== undefined) {
    const tagIds = tagIdsOf(v.tagIds, MAX_RULE_TAGS)
    if (tagIds === null || tagIds.length === 0) return null
    out.tagIds = tagIds
  }
  return out.categoryId === undefined && out.counterpartyId === undefined && out.tagIds === undefined ? null : out
}
```

Замінити `lib/domain/rules/usable.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import type { ActionTargets, Rule, RuleActions, UnusableAction } from './types'

/** The actions of a rule that cannot work now (shown as «неробоче»); [] when all of them can. */
export function unusableActions(rule: Pick<Rule, 'type' | 'actions'>, targets: ActionTargets): UnusableAction[] {
  const out: UnusableAction[] = []
  const { categoryId, counterpartyId, tagIds } = rule.actions
  if (categoryId !== undefined && targets.categories.get(categoryId) !== rule.type) out.push('category')
  if (counterpartyId !== undefined) {
    const counterparty = targets.counterparties.get(counterpartyId)
    if (counterparty === undefined || counterparty.archived) out.push('counterparty')
  }
  // The tag action works as a whole (S10t): one tag that is gone or archived makes it «неробоча».
  if (tagIds !== undefined && tagIds.some((id) => targets.tags.get(id)?.archived !== false)) out.push('tags')
  return out
}

/** The rules with their unusable actions removed: the other actions of such a rule still work. */
export function withoutUnusableActions(rules: readonly Rule[], targets: ActionTargets): Rule[] {
  return rules.map((rule) => {
    const unusable = unusableActions(rule, targets)
    if (unusable.length === 0) return rule
    const actions: RuleActions = { ...rule.actions }
    if (unusable.includes('category')) delete actions.categoryId
    if (unusable.includes('counterparty')) delete actions.counterpartyId
    if (unusable.includes('tags')) delete actions.tagIds
    return { ...rule, actions }
  })
}
```

`lib/domain/rules/match.ts`: після рядка `import type { LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'` додати:

```ts
import { mergeTags } from '@/lib/domain/tags/assign'
import { MAX_TX_TAGS } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
```

і замінити doc-коментар та функцію `applyRules`, а також функцію `ruleOperationOf`, на:

```ts
/**
 * What the rules give an operation. Each empty field (category, counterparty) is filled by the first matching rule that
 * has that action; a field that is already set is never touched. Tags (S10t) are added, not chosen: every matching rule
 * adds the tags the operation does not have yet, in rule order, while it has fewer than 10; no rule takes a tag off.
 * `applied` lists only the rules that gave something. Unusable actions must be removed before (`withoutUnusableActions`).
 */
export function applyRules(rules: readonly Rule[], op: RuleOperation): RuleOutcome {
  const out: RuleOutcome = { applied: [] }
  if (op.type === 'transfer') return out
  let needCategory = op.categoryId === undefined
  let needCounterparty = op.counterpartyId === undefined
  const own = op.tagIds ?? []
  let tags: string[] = [...own]
  const nothingLeft = () => !needCategory && !needCounterparty && tags.length >= MAX_TX_TAGS
  if (nothingLeft()) return out
  const texts = textsOf(op)
  for (const rule of sortRules(rules)) {
    if (nothingLeft()) break
    if (!matchesWith(rule, op, texts)) continue
    let filled = false
    if (needCategory && rule.actions.categoryId !== undefined) {
      out.categoryId = rule.actions.categoryId
      needCategory = false
      filled = true
    }
    if (needCounterparty && rule.actions.counterpartyId !== undefined) {
      out.counterpartyId = rule.actions.counterpartyId
      needCounterparty = false
      filled = true
    }
    if (rule.actions.tagIds !== undefined) {
      const merged = mergeTags(tags, rule.actions.tagIds)
      if (merged.length > tags.length) {
        tags = merged
        filled = true
      }
    }
    if (filled) out.applied.push({ id: rule.id, name: rule.name })
  }
  if (tags.length > own.length) out.tagIds = tags
  return out
}

/** A stored operation as the rules see it. */
export function ruleOperationOf(tx: LedgerTransaction): RuleOperation {
  const op: RuleOperation = { type: tx.type, accountId: tx.accountId, currency: tx.currency, amountMinor: tx.amountMinor }
  if (tx.bank?.remittance !== undefined) op.remittance = tx.bank.remittance
  if (tx.bank?.counterpartyName !== undefined) op.bankName = tx.bank.counterpartyName
  if (tx.description !== undefined) op.description = tx.description
  if (tx.categoryId !== undefined) op.categoryId = tx.categoryId
  if (tx.counterpartyId !== undefined) op.counterpartyId = tx.counterpartyId
  if (tx.tags.length > 0) op.tagIds = tx.tags
  return op
}
```

Замінити `lib/server/rules/read.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import type { Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { parseRule } from '@/lib/domain/rules/doc'
import { sortRules } from '@/lib/domain/rules/match'
import type { ActionTargets, ListedRule, Rule, RuleActions, RuleType } from '@/lib/domain/rules/types'
import { unusableActions, withoutUnusableActions } from '@/lib/domain/rules/usable'
import { categoriesCol, counterpartiesCol, rulesCol, tagsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'

/** Every readable rule of the company (at most 50) in the order they apply; a malformed document is left out. */
export async function readRules(db: Firestore, orgId: string): Promise<Rule[]> {
  const snap = await rulesCol(db, orgId).get()
  return sortRules(snap.docs.map((d) => parseRule(d.id, d.data())).filter((r): r is Rule => r !== null))
}

function idsOf(rules: readonly Rule[], pick: (actions: RuleActions) => readonly string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(rules.flatMap((r) => pick(r.actions)))]
}

const one = (id: string | undefined): string[] => (id === undefined ? [] : [id])

/** The categories, counterparties and tags the rules' actions name: one `getAll` each, none when nothing is named. */
export async function readTargets(db: Firestore, orgId: string, rules: readonly Rule[]): Promise<ActionTargets> {
  const categoryIds = idsOf(rules, (a) => one(a.categoryId))
  const counterpartyIds = idsOf(rules, (a) => one(a.counterpartyId))
  const tagIds = idsOf(rules, (a) => a.tagIds ?? [])
  const [categorySnaps, counterpartySnaps, tagSnaps] = await Promise.all([
    categoryIds.length === 0 ? Promise.resolve([]) : db.getAll(...categoryIds.map((id) => categoriesCol(db, orgId).doc(id))),
    counterpartyIds.length === 0 ? Promise.resolve([]) : db.getAll(...counterpartyIds.map((id) => counterpartiesCol(db, orgId).doc(id))),
    tagIds.length === 0 ? Promise.resolve([]) : db.getAll(...tagIds.map((id) => tagsCol(db, orgId).doc(id))),
  ])
  const categories = new Map<string, RuleType>()
  for (const s of categorySnaps) {
    const type: unknown = s.get('type')
    if (s.exists && (type === 'income' || type === 'expense')) categories.set(s.id, type)
  }
  const counterparties = new Map<string, { archived: boolean }>()
  for (const s of counterpartySnaps) if (s.exists) counterparties.set(s.id, { archived: s.get('archived') === true })
  const tags = new Map<string, { archived: boolean }>()
  for (const s of tagSnaps) if (s.exists) tags.set(s.id, { archived: s.get('archived') === true })
  return { categories, counterparties, tags }
}

/** `GET /rules`: every rule in order, with the actions that cannot work now. */
export async function listRules(db: Firestore, orgId: string): Promise<ListedRule[]> {
  const rules = await readRules(db, orgId)
  if (rules.length === 0) return []
  const targets = await readTargets(db, orgId, rules)
  return rules.map((rule) => ({ ...rule, unusable: unusableActions(rule, targets) }))
}

/** What a sync step or an apply step works with: the active rules, their unusable actions removed. */
export async function loadUsableRules(db: Firestore, orgId: string): Promise<Rule[]> {
  const active = (await readRules(db, orgId)).filter((r) => r.active)
  if (active.length === 0) return []
  return withoutUnusableActions(active, await readTargets(db, orgId, active))
}
```

`lib/server/rules/write.ts`:

1. Рядок `import { auditCol, categoriesCol, counterpartiesCol, orgRef, rulesCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'` замінити на:

```ts
import { auditCol, categoriesCol, counterpartiesCol, orgRef, rulesCol, tagsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'
```

2. Замінити функцію `assertTargets` з її коментарем на:

```ts
/**
 * The category must exist and have the rule's type; the counterparty must exist and not be archived; every tag the write
 * gives the action anew must exist and not be archived (S10t).
 */
function assertTargets(type: RuleType, category: DocumentSnapshot | null, counterparty: DocumentSnapshot | null, tags: readonly DocumentSnapshot[] = []): void {
  if (category !== null && (!category.exists || category.get('type') !== type)) throw new RuleError('category_not_found', 'actions.categoryId')
  if (counterparty !== null) {
    if (!counterparty.exists) throw new RuleError('counterparty_not_found', 'actions.counterpartyId')
    if (counterparty.get('archived') === true) throw new RuleError('counterparty_archived', 'actions.counterpartyId')
  }
  for (const tag of tags) {
    if (!tag.exists) throw new RuleError('tag_not_found', 'actions.tagIds')
    if (tag.get('archived') === true) throw new RuleError('tag_archived', 'actions.tagIds')
  }
}
```

```ts

    for (const id of input.actions.tagIds ?? []) tags.push(await t.get(tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(id)))
```

і рядок `    assertTargets(input.type, category, counterparty)` замінити на `    assertTargets(input.type, category, counterparty, tags)`.

```ts
    const addedTags = (actions.tagIds ?? []).filter((id) => !(prior.actions.tagIds ?? []).includes(id))
    const tags: DocumentSnapshot[] = []
    for (const id of addedTags) tags.push(await t.get(tagsCol(deps.db, orgId).doc(id)))
```

і рядок `    assertTargets(type, category, counterparty)` замінити на `    assertTargets(type, category, counterparty, tags)`.

Замінити `lib/server/rules/apply.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import 'server-only'
import { FieldPath, Timestamp, type Query, type QueryDocumentSnapshot } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { encodeCursor, type JournalKey } from '@/lib/domain/journal/order'
import { parseTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/doc'
import { LedgerError } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/errors'
import type { TransactionPatch } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/input'
import type { LedgerTransaction } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/types'
import { RuleError } from '@/lib/domain/rules/errors'
import { withCurrency, type RuleInput } from '@/lib/domain/rules/input'
import { applyRules, ruleOperationOf } from '@/lib/domain/rules/match'
import type { ApplyStepResult, CheckStepResult, PreviewResult, Rule } from '@/lib/domain/rules/types'
import { MAX_TX_TAGS } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { FxError } from '@/lib/server/fx/types'
import { keyOf } from '@/lib/server/journal/read'
import type { LedgerDeps } from '@/lib/server/ledger/deps'
import { orgRef, transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'
import { updateTransaction } from '@/lib/server/ledger/write'
import { loadUsableRules } from './read'

/** Operations one check or apply step reads, in the journal order. */
export const SCAN_PER_STEP = 200
/** Operations one apply step changes at most: one Firestore transaction each, as a bulk change of the journal. */
export const APPLY_PER_STEP = 25
/** Operations «Перевірити» reads at most, in one query. */
export const PREVIEW_CAP = 2000

/** Smaller steps for tests. */
export interface StepLimits {
  scan?: number
  apply?: number
}

/** The journal order (payment date, creation time, id, all descending): the unfiltered journal index serves it. */
function journalOrder(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string): Query {
  return transactionsCol(deps.db, orgId).orderBy('paymentDate', 'desc').orderBy('createdAt', 'desc').orderBy(FieldPath.documentId(), 'desc')
}

async function readChunk(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, cursor: JournalKey | null, size: number): Promise<QueryDocumentSnapshot[]> {
  const q = journalOrder(deps, orgId)
  const from = cursor === null ? q : q.startAfter(cursor.paymentDate, new Timestamp(cursor.createdAtS, cursor.createdAtN), cursor.id)
  return (await from.limit(size).get()).docs
}

/**
 * The end of a step. A step that could not place any document it read (no string payment date: a legacy document)
 * cannot move the cursor, so the scan stops there instead of reading the same documents again.
 */
function finish(docs: number, size: number, last: JournalKey | null, cursor: JournalKey | null): { done: boolean; cursor: string | null } {
  const done = docs < size || last === cursor
  return { done, cursor: done || last === null ? null : encodeCursor(last) }
}

/** One step of `check`: what the rules would give among the next SCAN_PER_STEP operations. Writes nothing. */
export async function checkStep(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, cursor: JournalKey | null, limits: StepLimits = {}): Promise<CheckStepResult> {
  const rules = await loadUsableRules(deps.db, orgId)
  const empty = { scanned: 0, matchOps: 0, categories: 0, counterparties: 0, tags: 0 }
  if (rules.length === 0) return { ...empty, done: true, cursor: null }
  const size = limits.scan ?? SCAN_PER_STEP
  const docs = await readChunk(deps, orgId, cursor, size)
  const counts = { ...empty }
  let last = cursor
  for (const d of docs) {
    counts.scanned++
    last = keyOf(d) ?? last
    const tx = parseTransaction(d.id, d.data())
    if (tx === null) continue
    const outcome = applyRules(rules, ruleOperationOf(tx))
    if (outcome.applied.length === 0) continue
    counts.matchOps++
    if (outcome.categoryId !== undefined) counts.categories++
    if (outcome.counterpartyId !== undefined) counts.counterparties++
    if (outcome.tagIds !== undefined) counts.tags++
  }
  return { ...counts, ...finish(docs.length, size, last, cursor) }
}

/**
 * One step of `apply`: gives what the rules give, at most APPLY_PER_STEP operations, each through `updateTransaction`
 * with the version this step read (`expectVersion`): a person's change after the read wins, the write is a `conflict`
 * and counts as failed. A ledger or rate refusal fails only that operation; anything else aborts the step.
 */
export async function applyStep(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, uid: string, cursor: JournalKey | null, limits: StepLimits = {}): Promise<ApplyStepResult> {
  const rules = await loadUsableRules(deps.db, orgId)
  if (rules.length === 0) return { scanned: 0, changed: 0, failed: 0, done: true, cursor: null }
  const size = limits.scan ?? SCAN_PER_STEP
  const most = limits.apply ?? APPLY_PER_STEP
  const docs = await readChunk(deps, orgId, cursor, size)
  let scanned = 0
  let changed = 0
  let failed = 0
  let last = cursor
  let full = false
  for (const d of docs) {
    if (changed + failed >= most) {
      full = true
      break
    }
    scanned++
    last = keyOf(d) ?? last
    const tx = parseTransaction(d.id, d.data())
    if (tx === null) continue
    const outcome = applyRules(rules, ruleOperationOf(tx))
    if (outcome.applied.length === 0) continue
    const patch: TransactionPatch = {}
    if (outcome.categoryId !== undefined) patch.categoryId = outcome.categoryId
    if (outcome.counterpartyId !== undefined) patch.counterpartyId = outcome.counterpartyId
    if (outcome.tagIds !== undefined) patch.tags = outcome.tagIds
    try {
      await updateTransaction(deps, orgId, uid, tx.id, patch, { autoRules: outcome.applied, expectVersion: d.updateTime })
      changed++
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof LedgerError || e instanceof FxError)) throw e
      failed++
    }
  }
  if (full) return { scanned, changed, failed, done: false, cursor: last === null ? null : encodeCursor(last) }
  return { scanned, changed, failed, ...finish(docs.length, size, last, cursor) }
}

/** An operation the draft rule could give something: an empty category or counterparty, or room for a tag of the rule it lacks (S10t). */
function couldGive(rule: Rule, tx: LedgerTransaction): boolean {
  if (tx.categoryId === undefined || tx.counterpartyId === undefined) return true
  return tx.tags.length < MAX_TX_TAGS && (rule.actions.tagIds ?? []).some((id) => !tx.tags.includes(id))
}

/**
 * «Перевірити»: reads the latest `cap` operations in one query and counts, among the ones the draft rule could give
 * something (`couldGive`), how many it would. Firestore cannot ask for a missing field, so the cap applies to the
 * operations read; `capped` says the query was full. Writes nothing; the rule's `active` is ignored.
 */
export async function previewRule(deps: LedgerDeps, orgId: string, input: RuleInput, cap = PREVIEW_CAP): Promise<PreviewResult> {
  const org = await orgRef(deps.db, orgId).get()
  if (!org.exists) throw new RuleError('not_found')
  const rule: Rule = {
    id: 'preview',
    name: input.name,
    type: input.type,
    active: true,
    order: 0,
    createdAtMs: 0,
    conditions: withCurrency(input.conditions, org.get('currency') as string),
    actions: input.actions,
  }
  const snap = await journalOrder(deps, orgId).limit(cap).get()
  let scanned = 0
  let matched = 0
  for (const d of snap.docs) {
    const tx = parseTransaction(d.id, d.data())
    if (tx === null || tx.type === 'transfer' || !couldGive(rule, tx)) continue
    scanned++
    if (applyRules([rule], ruleOperationOf(tx)).applied.length > 0) matched++
  }
  return { matched, scanned, capped: snap.size === cap }
}
```

`lib/server/banking/sync.ts`:

1. Рядок `const RULE_REFUSALS: ReadonlySet<LedgerErrorCode> = new Set(['category_not_found', 'counterparty_not_found', 'counterparty_archived'])` замінити на:

```ts
const RULE_REFUSALS: ReadonlySet<LedgerErrorCode> = new Set(['category_not_found', 'counterparty_not_found', 'counterparty_archived', 'tag_not_found', 'tag_archived'])
```

2. Замінити doc-коментар і функцію `createWithRules` на:

```ts
/**
 * Creates one imported operation with the category, the counterparty and the tags the rules give it (S10, S10t). The
 * rules were read at the start of the step; when the ledger refuses because of one of their actions (the category, the
 * counterparty or a tag changed since), the operation is written again without them: an operation is never lost because
 * of a rule.
 */
export async function createWithRules(
  deps: BankDeps,
  orgId: string,
  uid: string,
  input: TransactionInput,
  bank: BankInfo,
  currency: string,
  rules: readonly Rule[],
): Promise<WriteResult> {
  const outcome = applyRules(rules, {
    type: input.type,
    accountId: input.accountId,
    currency,
    amountMinor: input.amountMinor,
    remittance: bank.remittance,
    bankName: bank.counterpartyName,
    description: input.description,
    tagIds: input.tags,
  })
  if (outcome.applied.length === 0) return createTransaction(deps.ledger, orgId, uid, input, bank)
  const filled: TransactionInput = { ...input }
  if (outcome.categoryId !== undefined) filled.categoryId = outcome.categoryId
  if (outcome.counterpartyId !== undefined) filled.counterpartyId = outcome.counterpartyId
  if (outcome.tagIds !== undefined) filled.tags = outcome.tagIds
  try {
    return await createTransaction(deps.ledger, orgId, uid, filled, bank, { autoRules: outcome.applied })
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof LedgerError && RULE_REFUSALS.has(e.code)) return createTransaction(deps.ledger, orgId, uid, input, bank)
    throw e
  }
}
```

**Messages.** Створити інструментом Write `tmp-i18n.cjs` у корені репозиторію:

```js
// Temporary (S10t plan, Task 8): merges the keys below into the three catalogs, keeping their format. Not committed.
const fs = require('fs')
const ADD = {
  uk: {
    settings: {
      rules: {
        apply: {
          note: 'Правила заповнюють лише порожні поля, додають теги й не змінюють вибране вручну.',
          breakdown: 'Категорія: {categories}, контрагент: {counterparties}, теги: {tags}',
        },
      },
    },
  },
  pl: {
    settings: {
      rules: {
        apply: {
          note: 'Reguły uzupełniają tylko puste pola, dodają tagi i nie zmieniają tego, co wybrano ręcznie.',
          breakdown: 'Kategoria: {categories}, kontrahent: {counterparties}, tagi: {tags}',
        },
      },
    },
  },
  en: {
    settings: {
      rules: {
        apply: {
          note: 'The rules only fill empty fields, add tags and never change what was chosen by hand.',
          breakdown: 'Category: {categories}, counterparty: {counterparties}, tags: {tags}',
        },
      },
    },
  },
}
function merge(target, source) {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && target[key] !== null && typeof target[key] === 'object') merge(target[key], value)
    else target[key] = value
  }
}
for (const [lang, fragment] of Object.entries(ADD)) {
  const file = `messages/${lang}.json`
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
  const data = JSON.parse(src)
  merge(data, fragment)
  let out = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n'
  if (src.includes('\r\n')) out = out.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n')
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

const notify = vi.fn()
vi.mock('@/lib/api/ledger-events', () => ({ notifyLedgerChanged: () => notify() }))

import { LedgerApiError } from '@/lib/api/ledger-client'
import { createTag, duplicateTagOf, RecalcRunError, recalculateTag, tagImpact, updateTag } from '@/lib/api/tags-client'

const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
const TAG = { id: 'tg1', name: 'ПДВ', color: 'slate', excludeFromAnalytics: true, archived: false, recalcPending: false }

beforeEach(() => notify.mockClear())
afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals())

describe('tag calls', () => {
  it('create, edit and impact go to their paths', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ tag: TAG }, 201))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ tag: { ...TAG, recalcPending: true }, recalc: true }))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ operations: 12 }))
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
    expect(await createTag('org 1', { name: 'ПДВ', excludeFromAnalytics: true })).toEqual(TAG)
    expect(await updateTag('org 1', 'tg1', { excludeFromAnalytics: true })).toEqual({ tag: { ...TAG, recalcPending: true }, recalc: true })
    expect(await tagImpact('org 1', 'tg1')).toBe(12)
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls.map((c) => [c[0], c[1]?.method ?? 'GET'])).toEqual([
      ['/api/orgs/org%201/tags', 'POST'],
      ['/api/orgs/org%201/tags/tg1', 'PATCH'],
      ['/api/orgs/org%201/tags/tg1/impact', 'GET'],
    ])
    expect(JSON.parse(fetchMock.mock.calls[0][1].body)).toEqual({ name: 'ПДВ', excludeFromAnalytics: true })
  })

  it('a taken name is a duplicate with the id of the tag that has it', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(json({ error: 'duplicate', field: 'name', existingId: 'tg0' }, 409)))
    const error = await createTag('org1', { name: 'vat' }).catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(LedgerApiError)
    expect(duplicateTagOf(error)).toEqual({ existingId: 'tg0' })
    expect(duplicateTagOf(new LedgerApiError(409, 'limit_reached'))).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('recalculateTag', () => {
  it('asks step by step with the cursor until done, adds the counts and reloads the journal once', async () => {
    const fetchMock = vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ scanned: 100, changed: 100, done: false, cursor: 'tx-100' }))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ scanned: 20, changed: 19, done: true, cursor: null }))
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', fetchMock)
    const progress: number[] = []
    const totals = await recalculateTag('org1', 'tg1', { onProgress: (t) => progress.push(t.scanned) })
    expect(totals).toEqual({ scanned: 120, changed: 119, stoppedEarly: false })
    expect(progress).toEqual([100])
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe('/api/orgs/org1/tags/tg1/recalculate')
    expect(fetchMock.mock.calls.map((c) => JSON.parse(c[1].body))).toEqual([{}, { cursor: 'tx-100' }])
    expect(notify).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })

  it('a failed step keeps the counts read so far', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn()
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ scanned: 100, changed: 3, done: false, cursor: 'tx-100' }))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(json({ error: 'ledger_busy' }, 409)))
    const error = await recalculateTag('org1', 'tg1').catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(RecalcRunError)
    expect((error as RecalcRunError).totals).toEqual({ scanned: 100, changed: 3, stoppedEarly: true })
    expect(notify).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })

  it('nothing changed: the journal is left alone', async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(json({ scanned: 4, changed: 0, done: true, cursor: null })))
    expect(await recalculateTag('org1', 'tg1')).toEqual({ scanned: 4, changed: 0, stoppedEarly: false })
    expect(notify).not.toHaveBeenCalled()
  })
})
```

```ts
    // the counterparty and accrual now come from the form, never from the stored operation

      status: 'planned',

describe('an unchanged edit', () => {
  // Saving the form without touching anything must send exactly what undo would restore: the form-owned fields (tags
  // included) go through the state and back, the rest (project, files) is carried from the stored operation on both sides.
  const options = { today: '2026-10-28', crossCurrency: false }
```

Створити `lib/api/tags-client.ts`:

```ts
import type { TagInput, TagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import type { RecalcStepResult, Tag, TagImpact, UpdateTagResult } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { notifyLedgerChanged } from './ledger-events'
import { apiCall, LedgerApiError, readApiError } from './ledger-client'

/** A recalculation step reads 100 operations: this only stops a runaway loop (100 000 operations). */
const MAX_STEPS = 1000

const base = (orgId: string) => `/api/orgs/${encodeURIComponent(orgId)}/tags`

/** A create body: the server fills the colour (slate) and the flag (off) when they are left out. */
export type TagDraft = Pick<TagInput, 'name'> & Partial<Omit<TagInput, 'name'>>

export async function createTag(orgId: string, draft: TagDraft): Promise<Tag> {
  return ((await apiCall('POST', base(orgId), draft)) as { tag: Tag }).tag
}

/** `recalc: true` means the operations of the tag must now be recalculated (`recalculateTag`). */
export async function updateTag(orgId: string, tagId: string, patch: TagPatch): Promise<UpdateTagResult> {
  return (await apiCall('PATCH', `${base(orgId)}/${encodeURIComponent(tagId)}`, patch)) as UpdateTagResult
}

/** How many operations carry the tag: what a change of its flag recalculates. */
export async function tagImpact(orgId: string, tagId: string): Promise<number> {
  return ((await apiCall('GET', `${base(orgId)}/${encodeURIComponent(tagId)}/impact`)) as TagImpact).operations
}

export interface RecalcTotals {
  scanned: number
  changed: number
  /** The step cap ended the run before done. */
  stoppedEarly: boolean
}

/** A step failed after earlier steps: the counts so far stay with the error (`cause` is the step's error). */
export class RecalcRunError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly totals: RecalcTotals,
    public readonly cause: unknown,
  ) {
    super('tag recalculation failed')
    this.name = 'RecalcRunError'
  }
}

/** Runs the recalculation of a tag's operations to the end, step by step; the journal reloads once when something changed. */
export async function recalculateTag(
  orgId: string,
  tagId: string,
  opts: { signal?: AbortSignal; onProgress?: (totals: RecalcTotals) => void } = {},
): Promise<RecalcTotals> {
  const totals: RecalcTotals = { scanned: 0, changed: 0, stoppedEarly: true }
  let cursor: string | null = null
  try {
    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_STEPS; i++) {
      let answer: RecalcStepResult
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`${base(orgId)}/${encodeURIComponent(tagId)}/recalculate`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'same-origin',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(cursor === null ? {} : { cursor }),
          signal: opts.signal,
        })
        if (!res.ok) throw await readApiError(res)
        answer = (await res.json()) as RecalcStepResult
      } catch (err) {
        throw new RecalcRunError({ ...totals }, err)
      }
      totals.scanned += answer.scanned
      totals.changed += answer.changed
      if (answer.done || answer.cursor === null) {
        totals.stoppedEarly = false
        break
      }
      cursor = answer.cursor
      opts.onProgress?.({ ...totals })
    }
  } finally {
    if (totals.changed > 0) notifyLedgerChanged()
  }
  return totals
}

/** The tag behind a `duplicate` answer (another tag has the name), or null for any other error. */
export function duplicateTagOf(e: unknown): { existingId: string | undefined } | null {
  if (!(e instanceof LedgerApiError) || e.code !== 'duplicate') return null
  const existingId = e.details?.existingId
  return { existingId: typeof existingId === 'string' ? existingId : undefined }
}
```

Створити `lib/firebase/tags.ts`:

```ts
import { collection, onSnapshot, type Unsubscribe } from 'firebase/firestore'
import { parseTag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/doc'
import { sortTags } from '@/lib/domain/tags/search'
import type { Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { db } from './firebase'

/** Tags are written by the server only (lib/api/tags-client.ts); the browser reads them here, live, in name order. */
export function getTags(orgId: string, callback: (tags: Tag[]) => void): Unsubscribe {
  return onSnapshot(
    collection(db, 'organizations', orgId, 'tags'),
    (snap) => {
      const list: Tag[] = []
      let skipped = 0
      for (const d of snap.docs) {
        const tag = parseTag(d.id, d.data())
        if (tag) list.push(tag)
        else skipped++
      }
      if (skipped > 0) console.warn(`tags: skipped ${skipped} unreadable document(s) in ${orgId}`)
      callback(sortTags(list))
    },
    (error) => {
      console.warn(`tags: cannot read ${orgId} (${error.code})`)
      callback([])
    },
  )
}
```

Створити `lib/hooks/useTags.ts`:

```ts
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { getTags } from '@/lib/firebase/tags'
import type { Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'

const EMPTY: Tag[] = []

export interface UseTagsResult {
  tags: Tag[]
  loading: boolean
}

/** The company's tags, live and in name order; the archived ones too (old operations still show them). */
export function useTags(orgId: string | null): UseTagsResult {
  // Stored with its company, so a company switch never shows the previous company's tags while the new
  // subscription waits for its first snapshot (same approach as useCounterparties).
  const [snapshot, setSnapshot] = useState<{ orgId: string; list: Tag[] } | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!orgId) return
    return getTags(orgId, (list) => setSnapshot({ orgId, list }))
  }, [orgId])

  const current = orgId !== null && snapshot?.orgId === orgId
  return { tags: current ? snapshot.list : EMPTY, loading: orgId !== null && !current }
}
```

Замінити `lib/api/transaction-draft.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import type { TransactionDraft } from '@/lib/api/ledger-client'
import { accrualFor } from '@/lib/domain/transaction-form/accrual'
import type { FormState } from '@/lib/domain/transaction-form/state'
import { effectiveStatus } from '@/lib/domain/transaction-form/status'
import { parseAmountInput } from '@/lib/utils/amount-input'
import type { Transaction } from '@/types'

/** What the transaction form edits itself: everything in a draft except what it has no control for (project, files). */
export type FormOwnedDraft = Omit<TransactionDraft, 'projectId' | 'fileUrls'>

/** The `YYYY-MM-DD` UTC day of a stored payment date. */
function paymentDay(date: Date): string {
  return date.toISOString().split('T')[0]
}

/** The draft the form sends for what is typed. `validateState` has already refused anything unparseable. */
export function stateToDraft(state: FormState, o: { today: string; crossCurrency: boolean }): FormOwnedDraft {
  const draft: FormOwnedDraft = {
    type: state.type,
    status: effectiveStatus(state.status, state.paymentDate, o.today),
    accountId: state.accountId,
    amount: parseAmountInput(state.amount) ?? 0,
    paymentDate: state.paymentDate,
    isRecurring: state.isRecurring,
    tags: [...state.tagIds],
  }
  if (state.type === 'transfer') {
    draft.toAccountId = state.toAccountId
    if (o.crossCurrency) draft.toAmount = parseAmountInput(state.toAmount) ?? 0
  } else {
    if (state.categoryId) draft.categoryId = state.categoryId
    if (state.counterpartyId) draft.counterpartyId = state.counterpartyId
    const accrual = accrualFor(state.accrual, state.paymentDate)
    if (accrual) draft.accrual = accrual
  }
  const description = state.description.trim()
  if (description) draft.description = description
  return draft
}

/** A draft for a new operation: it has no files or project yet. */
export function buildCreateDraft(form: FormOwnedDraft): TransactionDraft {
  return { ...form, fileUrls: [] }
}

/**
 * A draft for saving an edited operation. The edit is sent as a full replace (absent optional fields are
 * cleared), so the two fields the form has no control for must be carried over from the stored operation,
 * or an edit of the amount alone would erase them: project and files. Everything else (category, counterparty,
 * accrual, status, description, tags since S10t) comes from the form, so the person can still change or clear it.
 */
export function buildEditDraft(transaction: Transaction, form: FormOwnedDraft): TransactionDraft {
  const draft: TransactionDraft = {
    ...form,
    fileUrls: [...transaction.fileUrls],
  }
  if (transaction.projectId !== undefined) draft.projectId = transaction.projectId
  return draft
}

/**
 * The full draft of a stored operation: what a create (restoring a deleted operation) or a full replace
 * (undoing an edit) needs to give the same operation back. An accrual that merely followed the payment date
 * is left out so it follows again. A transfer has neither accrual nor counterparty (the server refuses both),
 * and its second amount only matters across currencies.
 */
export function transactionToDraft(tx: Transaction): TransactionDraft {
  const draft: TransactionDraft = {
    type: tx.type,
    status: tx.status,
    accountId: tx.accountId,
    amount: tx.amount,
    paymentDate: paymentDay(tx.date),
    isRecurring: tx.isRecurring,
    tags: [...tx.tags],
    fileUrls: [...tx.fileUrls],
  }
  if (tx.description !== undefined) draft.description = tx.description
  if (tx.projectId !== undefined) draft.projectId = tx.projectId
  if (tx.type === 'transfer') {
    if (tx.toAccountId !== undefined) draft.toAccountId = tx.toAccountId
    if (tx.toAmount !== undefined && tx.toCurrency !== undefined && tx.toCurrency !== tx.currency) draft.toAmount = tx.toAmount
    return draft
  }
  if (tx.categoryId !== undefined) draft.categoryId = tx.categoryId
  if (tx.counterpartyId !== undefined) draft.counterpartyId = tx.counterpartyId
  const followedPaymentDate = tx.accrual.mode === 'date' && tx.accrual.date === paymentDay(tx.date)
  if (!followedPaymentDate) draft.accrual = tx.accrual
  return draft
}

/** The category after the user picks another type tab: a category belongs to one type, so it is cleared on a real change only. */
export function categoryAfterTypeChange(
  categoryId: string,
  from: Transaction['type'],
  to: Transaction['type'],
): string {
  return from === to ? categoryId : ''
}
```
