```tsx
'use client'

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { TagDot } from '@/components/tags/TagDot'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { LedgerApiError } from '@/lib/api/ledger-client'
import { createTag, tagImpact, updateTag } from '@/lib/api/tags-client'
import type { TagPatch } from '@/lib/domain/tags/input'
import { DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR, TAG_COLORS, TAG_NAME_MAX, type Tag, type TagColor } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface Props {
  orgId: string
  /** The tag to edit; absent for a new one. */
  tag?: Tag
  onClose: () => void
  /** After a change of the flag of a tag that some operation carries: the page runs the recalculation. */
  onRecalc: (tag: Tag) => void
}

type Phase = { kind: 'form' } | { kind: 'confirm'; count: number; patch: TagPatch }

/**
 * The tag editor: name, a colour of the palette, «Не враховувати в аналітиці», archive or restore. A change of the
 * flag of a tag with operations asks first («Буде перераховано операцій: N»), then the page shows the recalculation.
 */
export function TagEditor({ orgId, tag, onClose, onRecalc }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations('settings.tags')
  const tCommon = useTranslations('common')
  const [name, setName] = useState(tag?.name ?? '')
  const [color, setColor] = useState<TagColor>(tag?.color ?? DEFAULT_TAG_COLOR)
  const [exclude, setExclude] = useState(tag?.excludeFromAnalytics ?? false)
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>({ kind: 'form' })
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)

  const errorText = (e: unknown): string => {
    const code = e instanceof LedgerApiError ? e.code : ''
    if (code === 'duplicate') return t('errors.duplicate')
    if (code === 'limit_reached') return t('errors.limit')
    if (code === 'not_found') return t('errors.gone')
    if (code === 'invalid_input') return t('errors.invalid')
    return t('errors.save')
  }

  /** The fields that differ from the stored tag. */
  const changes = (): TagPatch => {
    const patch: TagPatch = {}
    if (!tag) return patch
    if (name.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() !== tag.name) patch.name = name
    if (color !== tag.color) patch.color = color
    if (exclude !== tag.excludeFromAnalytics) patch.excludeFromAnalytics = exclude
    return patch
  }

  const send = async (patch: TagPatch) => {
    if (!tag) return
    const result = await updateTag(orgId, tag.id, patch)
    toast.success(t('saved'))
    onClose()
    if (result.recalc) onRecalc(result.tag)
  }

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (busy || name.trim() === '') return
    setBusy(true)
    try {
      if (!tag) {
        await createTag(orgId, { name, color, excludeFromAnalytics: exclude })
        toast.success(t('saved'))
        onClose()
        return
      }
      const patch = changes()
      if (Object.keys(patch).length === 0) {
        onClose()
        return
      }
      if (patch.excludeFromAnalytics !== undefined) {
        const count = await tagImpact(orgId, tag.id)
        if (count > 0) {
          setPhase({ kind: 'confirm', count, patch })
          setBusy(false)
          return
        }
      }
      await send(patch)
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(errorText(err))
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const confirm = async () => {
    if (phase.kind !== 'confirm') return
    setBusy(true)
    try {
      await send(phase.patch)
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(errorText(err))
      setBusy(false)
      setPhase({ kind: 'form' })
    }
  }

  const toggleArchive = async () => {
    if (!tag || busy) return
    setBusy(true)
    try {
      await updateTag(orgId, tag.id, { archived: !tag.archived })
      toast.success(t('saved'))
      onClose()
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(errorText(err))
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  if (phase.kind === 'confirm') {
    return (
      <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => !open && !busy && setPhase({ kind: 'form' })}>
        <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="sm:max-w-md">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>{t('editor.confirmTitle')}</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription data-testid="tag-recalc-count">{t('editor.confirmBody', { count: phase.count })}</DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button type="button" variant="outline" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setPhase({ kind: 'form' })}>
              {tCommon('cancel')}
            </Button>
            <Button type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void confirm()}>
              {t('editor.confirm')}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => !open && !busy && onClose()}>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[90dvh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{tag ? t('editor.titleEdit') : t('editor.titleNew')}</DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>
        <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <Label htmlFor="tag-name">{t('editor.name')}</Label>
            <Input id="tag-name" maxLength={TAG_NAME_MAX} value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} />
          </div>

          <fieldset className="space-y-2">
            <legend className="text-sm font-medium text-ink">{t('editor.color')}</legend>
            <div role="radiogroup" aria-label={t('editor.color')} className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {TAG_COLORS.map((c) => (
                <button
                  key={c}
                  type="button"
                  role="radio"
                  aria-checked={color === c}
                  aria-label={t(`colors.${c}`)}
                  onClick={() => setColor(c)}
                  className={cn(
                    'flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border bg-surface focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-brand-soft-foreground',
                    color === c ? 'border-ink' : 'border-line',
                  )}
                >
                  <TagDot color={c} className="size-4" />
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </fieldset>

          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <input
              id="tag-exclude"
              type="checkbox"
              role="switch"
              className="mt-1 accent-brand"
              checked={exclude}
              onChange={(e) => setExclude(e.target.checked)}
              aria-describedby="tag-exclude-hint"
            />
            <div className="space-y-0.5">
              <Label htmlFor="tag-exclude">{t('editor.exclude')}</Label>
              <p id="tag-exclude-hint" className="text-xs text-ink-muted">
                {t('editor.excludeHint')}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {tag?.archived && <p className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{t('editor.archivedNote')}</p>}

          <DialogFooter className="flex-row items-center justify-between gap-2">
            {tag ? (
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void toggleArchive()}>
                {tag.archived ? t('editor.restore') : t('editor.archive')}
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <span />
            )}
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={onClose} disabled={busy}>
                {tCommon('cancel')}
              </Button>
              <Button type="submit" disabled={busy || name.trim() === ''}>
                {busy ? t('editor.saving') : tCommon('save')}
              </Button>
            </div>
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}
```

Створити `components/tags/TagRecalcDialog.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { RecalcRunError, recalculateTag } from '@/lib/api/tags-client'
import type { Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'

interface Props {
  orgId: string
  tag: Tag
  onClose: () => void
}

type Phase = { kind: 'running'; scanned: number } | { kind: 'done'; scanned: number } | { kind: 'failed' }

/**
 * Runs the recalculation of a tag's operations on mount, with the progress. Closing stops after the step in flight (a
 * step is safe to repeat); the tag then keeps «Перерахунок не завершено» and the list offers «Продовжити».
 */
export function TagRecalcDialog({ orgId, tag, onClose }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations('settings.tags.recalc')
  const tCommon = useTranslations('common')
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>({ kind: 'running', scanned: 0 })
  const running = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    running.current = controller
    recalculateTag(orgId, tag.id, { signal: controller.signal, onProgress: (p) => setPhase({ kind: 'running', scanned: p.scanned }) }).then(
      (totals) => setPhase({ kind: 'done', scanned: totals.scanned }),
      (err: unknown) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted && err instanceof RecalcRunError) setPhase({ kind: 'failed' })
      },
    )
    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [orgId, tag.id])

  const close = () => {
    running.current?.abort()
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(open) => !open && close()}>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{t('title')}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>{tag.name}</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <div data-testid="tag-recalc-state" aria-live="polite" className="text-sm text-ink">
          {phase.kind === 'running' && <p>{t('running', { scanned: phase.scanned })}</p>}
          {phase.kind === 'done' && <p className="font-medium">{t('done', { scanned: phase.scanned })}</p>}
          {phase.kind === 'failed' && <p className="text-expense">{t('failed')}</p>}
        </div>
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button data-testid="tag-recalc-close" type="button" variant="outline" onClick={close}>
            {phase.kind === 'running' ? tCommon('cancel') : tCommon('close')}
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}
```

Створити `app/(app)/[orgSlug]/settings/tags/page.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { Pencil, Plus } from 'lucide-react'
import { TagDot } from '@/components/tags/TagDot'
import { TagEditor } from '@/components/tags/TagEditor'
import { TagRecalcDialog } from '@/components/tags/TagRecalcDialog'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Card, CardContent, CardDescription, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import { MAX_TAGS, type Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { useShell } from '@/lib/hooks/useShell'
import { useTags } from '@/lib/hooks/useTags'

export default function TagsSettingsPage() {
  const t = useTranslations('settings.tags')
  const { orgId, can } = useShell()
  const canEdit = can('data.write')
  const { tags, loading } = useTags(orgId)
  // 'new': the editor of a new tag; a tag: its editor.
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Tag | 'new' | null>(null)
  // The tag whose operations are being recalculated (after a flag change, or «Продовжити»).
  const [recalc, setRecalc] = useState<Tag | null>(null)
  const active = tags.filter((tag) => !tag.archived)
  const archived = tags.filter((tag) => tag.archived)
  const full = tags.length >= MAX_TAGS

  const row = (tag: Tag) => (
    <li key={tag.id} data-testid="tag-row" className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-3">
      <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <TagDot color={tag.color} />
          <span className={tag.archived ? 'font-medium text-ink-muted' : 'font-medium text-ink'}>{tag.name}</span>
          {tag.excludeFromAnalytics && (
            <Badge variant="chip" data-testid="tag-excluded">
              {t('excludedBadge')}
            </Badge>
          )}
          {tag.archived && <Badge variant="chip">{t('archivedBadge')}</Badge>}
        </div>
        {/* Visible text, not a tooltip: a phone has no hover. */}
        {tag.recalcPending && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-expense">
            <span data-testid="tag-recalc-pending">{t('recalcPending')}</span>
            {canEdit && (
              <Button type="button" variant="soft" size="sm" onClick={() => setRecalc(tag)}>
                {t('recalcContinue')}
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      {canEdit && (
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={t('edit', { name: tag.name })} onClick={() => setEditing(tag)}>
          <Pencil />
        </Button>
      )}
    </li>
  )

  return (
    <Card className="ring-line">
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <CardTitle className="text-base font-semibold text-ink">{t('title')}</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>{t('description')}</CardDescription>
        </div>
        {canEdit && (
          <Button onClick={() => setEditing('new')} disabled={loading || full}>
            <Plus />
            {t('add')}
          </Button>
        )}
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-4">
        {loading ? null : tags.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-muted">{t('empty')}</p>
        ) : (
          <>
            {active.length > 0 && <ul className="divide-y divide-line">{active.map(row)}</ul>}
            {archived.length > 0 && (
              <section className="space-y-1">
                <h2 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-muted">{t('archivedTitle')}</h2>
                <ul className="divide-y divide-line">{archived.map(row)}</ul>
              </section>
            )}
          </>
        )}
        {canEdit && full && <p className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{t('limitHint')}</p>}
      </CardContent>

      {editing !== null && (
        <TagEditor
          key={editing === 'new' ? 'new' : editing.id}
          orgId={orgId}
          tag={editing === 'new' ? undefined : editing}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onRecalc={setRecalc}
        />
      )}
      {recalc !== null && <TagRecalcDialog key={recalc.id} orgId={orgId} tag={recalc} onClose={() => setRecalc(null)} />}
    </Card>
  )
}
```

```js
}
function merge(target, source) {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && target[key] !== null && typeof target[key] === 'object') merge(target[key], value)
    else target[key] = value
  }
}
for (const [lang, fragment] of Object.entries(ADD)) {
  const file = `messages/${lang}.json`
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
  const data = JSON.parse(src)
  merge(data, fragment)
  let out = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n'
  if (src.includes('\r\n')) out = out.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n')
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

```js
// Temporary (S10t plan, Task 11): merges the keys below into the three catalogs, keeping their format. Not committed.
const fs = require('fs')
const ADD = {
  uk: {
    settings: {
      tags: {
        picker: {
          label: 'Теги',
          placeholder: 'Почніть вводити назву тегу',
          create: 'Створити «{name}»',
          noMatches: 'Тегів не знайдено',
          remove: 'Прибрати тег «{name}»',
          full: 'Не більше {max} тегів',
          excluded: 'не враховується в аналітиці',
        },
      },
    },
    forms: {
      transaction: {
        tags: 'Теги',
        errors: {
          tagArchived: 'Тег в архіві. Приберіть його з операції',
          tagMissing: 'Тег не знайдено',
          tagCreate: 'Не вдалося створити тег',
        },
      },
    },
  },
  pl: {
    settings: {
      tags: {
        picker: {
          label: 'Tagi',
          placeholder: 'Zacznij wpisywać nazwę tagu',
          create: 'Utwórz „{name}”',
          noMatches: 'Nie znaleziono tagów',
          remove: 'Usuń tag „{name}”',
          full: 'Nie więcej niż {max} tagów',
          excluded: 'nie wliczany do analityki',
        },
      },
    },
    forms: {
      transaction: {
        tags: 'Tagi',
        errors: {
          tagArchived: 'Tag jest w archiwum. Usuń go z operacji',
          tagMissing: 'Nie znaleziono tagu',
          tagCreate: 'Nie udało się utworzyć tagu',
        },
      },
    },
  },
  en: {
    settings: {
      tags: {
        picker: {
          label: 'Tags',
          placeholder: 'Start typing a tag name',
          create: 'Create “{name}”',
          noMatches: 'No tags found',
          remove: 'Remove the tag “{name}”',
          full: 'At most {max} tags',
          excluded: 'not counted in analytics',
        },
      },
    },
    forms: {
      transaction: {
        tags: 'Tags',
        errors: {
          tagArchived: 'The tag is archived. Remove it from the transaction',
          tagMissing: 'The tag was not found',
          tagCreate: 'Could not create the tag',
        },
      },
    },
  },
}
function merge(target, source) {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && target[key] !== null && typeof target[key] === 'object') merge(target[key], value)
    else target[key] = value
  }
}
for (const [lang, fragment] of Object.entries(ADD)) {
  const file = `messages/${lang}.json`
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
  const data = JSON.parse(src)
  merge(data, fragment)
  let out = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n'
  if (src.includes('\r\n')) out = out.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n')
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

Створити `components/tags/TagPicker.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useId, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { EyeOff, Plus, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { TagDot } from '@/components/tags/TagDot'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { pickableTags, tagByName } from '@/lib/domain/tags/search'
import { MAX_TX_TAGS, type Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

const MAX_OPTIONS = 50

interface Props {
  id?: string
  /** The company's tags (archived ones too: a chosen archived tag still shows as a chip). */
  tags: Tag[]
  /** The chosen tag ids, in order. */
  value: string[]
  onChange: (ids: string[]) => void
  /** How many may be chosen. */
  max?: number
  /** Offer archived tags too (taking tags off in the journal). */
  includeArchived?: boolean
  /** Offer only these ids (the tags the selected operations have). */
  only?: readonly string[]
  /** When given, a name no tag has can be created; resolves to the new (or the existing) tag id, or null when it failed. */
  onCreate?: (name: string) => Promise<string | null>
  disabled?: boolean
}

type Row = { kind: 'tag'; tag: Tag } | { kind: 'create'; name: string }

/** A multiple choice of tags: the chosen ones as chips, a searchable list, and «Створити «…»» when allowed. */
export function TagPicker({ id, tags, value, onChange, max = MAX_TX_TAGS, includeArchived = false, only, onCreate, disabled }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations('settings.tags.picker')
  const listId = useId()
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0)
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
  // Where the pointer last was over an option: only a real move may move the highlight (see CounterpartyPicker).
  const pointer = useRef<string | null>(null)

  const byId = useMemo(() => new Map(tags.map((tag) => [tag.id, tag])), [tags])
  const offered = useMemo(() => {
    const pool = only === undefined ? tags : tags.filter((tag) => only.includes(tag.id))
    return pickableTags(pool, query, value, { includeArchived }).slice(0, MAX_OPTIONS)
  }, [tags, only, query, value, includeArchived])
  const full = value.length >= max
  const typed = query.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
  const canCreate = onCreate !== undefined && typed !== '' && tagByName(tags, typed) === undefined
  const rows: Row[] = [...offered.map((tag): Row => ({ kind: 'tag', tag })), ...(canCreate ? [{ kind: 'create', name: typed } as Row] : [])]
  const highlighted = Math.min(active, Math.max(rows.length - 1, 0))

  const close = () => {
    setOpen(false)
    setQuery('')
    setActive(0)
    pointer.current = null
  }

  const choose = async (row: Row) => {
    if (row.kind === 'tag') {
      onChange([...value, row.tag.id])
      setQuery('')
      setActive(0)
      return
    }
    if (!onCreate || creating) return
    setCreating(true)
    try {
      const created = await onCreate(row.name)
      if (created !== null && !value.includes(created)) onChange([...value, created])
      setQuery('')
      setActive(0)
    } finally {
      setCreating(false)
    }
  }

  const remove = (tagId: string) => onChange(value.filter((x) => x !== tagId))

  const onKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
      e.preventDefault()
      if (open) setActive(Math.min(highlighted + 1, Math.max(rows.length - 1, 0)))
      else setOpen(true)
    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
      e.preventDefault()
      setActive(Math.max(highlighted - 1, 0))
    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && open && rows.length > 0) {
      e.preventDefault()
      void choose(rows[highlighted])
    } else if (e.key === 'Escape' && open) {
      e.preventDefault()
      // Closing the list must not also close the dialog around it.
      e.stopPropagation()
      close()
    } else if (e.key === 'Backspace' && query === '' && value.length > 0) {
      remove(value[value.length - 1])
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {value.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5" aria-label={t('label')}>
          {value.map((tagId) => {
            const tag = byId.get(tagId)
            if (!tag) return null
            return (
              <li
                key={tagId}
                data-testid="tag-picked"
                className={cn('inline-flex h-7 max-w-full items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control border border-line bg-canvas pr-1 pl-2 text-sm', tag.archived ? 'text-ink-muted' : 'text-ink')}
              >
                <TagDot color={tag.color} />
                <span className="max-w-40 truncate">{tag.name}</span>
                {tag.excludeFromAnalytics && (
                  <>
                    <EyeOff className="size-3.5 text-ink-muted" aria-hidden />
                    <span className="sr-only">{t('excluded')}</span>
                  </>
                )}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={disabled}
                  onClick={() => remove(tagId)}
                  aria-label={t('remove', { name: tag.name })}
                  className="flex size-5 items-center justify-center rounded-[4px] text-ink-muted hover:bg-line hover:text-ink focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-brand-soft-foreground"
                >
                  <X className="size-3.5" aria-hidden />
                </button>
              </li>
            )
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
      <div className="relative">
        <Input
          id={id}
          role="combobox"
          aria-expanded={open && !full}
          aria-controls={listId}
          aria-autocomplete="list"
          aria-activedescendant={open && !full && rows.length > 0 ? `${listId}-${highlighted}` : undefined}
          aria-label={t('label')}
          placeholder={full ? t('full', { max }) : t('placeholder')}
          disabled={disabled || full || creating}
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setQuery(e.target.value)
            setActive(0)
            setOpen(true)
          }}
          onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
          onBlur={close}
          onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
        />
        {open && !full && (
          <ul
            id={listId}
            role="listbox"
            aria-label={t('label')}
            className="absolute z-20 mt-1 max-h-64 w-full overflow-y-auto rounded-control border border-line bg-surface py-1 shadow-panel"
          >
            {rows.map((row, index) => (
              <li
                key={row.kind === 'tag' ? row.tag.id : 'create'}
                id={`${listId}-${index}`}
                role="option"
                aria-selected={index === highlighted}
                // mousedown (not click) so the input's blur does not close the list before the choice is made.
                onMouseDown={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault()
                  void choose(row)
                }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                  pointer.current = `${e.screenX},${e.screenY}`
                }}
                onMouseMove={(e) => {
                  const at = `${e.screenX},${e.screenY}`
                  if (at === pointer.current) return
                  pointer.current = at
                  setActive(index)
                }}
                className={cn('flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm', index === highlighted && 'bg-canvas')}
              >
                {row.kind === 'tag' ? (
                  <>
                    <TagDot color={row.tag.color} />
                    <span className={cn('min-w-0 flex-1 truncate', row.tag.archived && 'text-ink-muted')}>{row.tag.name}</span>
                    {row.tag.excludeFromAnalytics && <span className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{t('excluded')}</span>}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Plus className="size-4" aria-hidden />
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{t('create', { name: row.name })}</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </li>
            ))}
            {rows.length === 0 && (
              <li role="presentation" className="px-3 py-2 text-sm text-ink-muted">
                {t('noMatches')}
              </li>
            )}
          </ul>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Створити `components/forms/transaction/TagsField.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { toast } from 'sonner'
import { TagPicker } from '@/components/tags/TagPicker'
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label'
import { createTag, duplicateTagOf } from '@/lib/api/tags-client'
import { MAX_TX_TAGS, type Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'

interface Props {
  orgId: string
  tags: Tag[]
  value: string[]
  onChange: (ids: string[]) => void
  /** The owner and the accountant may create a tag right from the field. */
  canCreate: boolean
}

/** «Теги» of the operation form (S10t): several tags; a name nobody has yet creates the tag at once. */
export function TagsField({ orgId, tags, value, onChange, canCreate }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations('forms.transaction')

  // The tag is created now (the operation is saved later); a name another tag already has picks that tag.
  const create = async (name: string): Promise<string | null> => {
    try {
      return (await createTag(orgId, { name })).id
    } catch (e) {
      const duplicate = duplicateTagOf(e)
      if (duplicate?.existingId) return duplicate.existingId
      toast.error(t('errors.tagCreate'))
      return null
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1">
      <Label htmlFor="tx-tags">{t('tags')}</Label>
      <TagPicker id="tx-tags" tags={tags} value={value} onChange={onChange} max={MAX_TX_TAGS} onCreate={canCreate ? create : undefined} />
    </div>
  )
}
```

Створити `components/tags/TagChips.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import { EyeOff } from 'lucide-react'
import { TagDot } from '@/components/tags/TagDot'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import type { Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

interface Props {
  ids: readonly string[]
  tagsById: ReadonlyMap<string, Tag>
  /** `tag-chip` in the comment column, `tag-chip-phone` under the account on a phone. */
  testId: string
  className?: string
}

/** The tags of one journal row: colour, name, and a mark on a tag that keeps the operation out of the analytics. */
export function TagChips({ ids, tagsById, testId, className }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations('settings.tags.picker')
  const known = ids.flatMap((id) => {
    const tag = tagsById.get(id)
    return tag ? [tag] : []
  })
  if (known.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <span className={cn('flex flex-wrap gap-1', className)}>
      {known.map((tag) => (
        <Badge key={tag.id} variant="chip" data-testid={testId} className={cn('max-w-40 gap-1 font-normal', tag.archived && 'text-ink-muted')}>
          <TagDot color={tag.color} />
          <span className="truncate">{tag.name}</span>
          {tag.excludeFromAnalytics && (
            <>
              <EyeOff className="size-3 text-ink-muted" aria-hidden />
              <span className="sr-only">{t('excluded')}</span>
            </>
          )}
        </Badge>
      ))}
    </span>
  )
}
```

```tsx
                  <span>{t('untagged')}</span>
                </label>
                {tags.map((tag) => (
                  <label key={tag.id} className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <input type="checkbox" className="accent-brand" checked={filters.tagIds.includes(tag.id)} onChange={() => toggleTag(tag.id)} />
                    <TagDot color={tag.color} />
                    <span className={tag.archived ? 'text-ink-muted' : undefined}>{tag.name}</span>
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
              <label className="mt-2 flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <input type="checkbox" className="accent-brand" checked={filters.excludedOnly} onChange={(e) => set({ excludedOnly: e.target.checked })} />
                <span>{t('excludedOnly')}</span>
              </label>
            </fieldset>
          )}
```

```js
    },
  },
  pl: {
    journal: {
      filters: { tags: 'Tagi', untagged: 'Bez tagów', excludedOnly: 'Tylko niewliczane do analityki' },
      selection: { field_addTags: 'Dodaj tag', field_removeTags: 'Zdejmij tag' },
      bulk: {
        title_addTags: 'Dodawanie tagów',
        title_removeTags: 'Zdejmowanie tagów',
        tagsAddHint: 'Tagi dochodzą do istniejących, najwyżej 10 na operację.',
        tagsNone: 'Wybrane operacje nie mają tagów',
        errors: { tag_not_found: 'Nie znaleziono tagu', tag_archived: 'Tag jest w archiwum' },
      },
      export: { column_tags: 'Tagi' },
    },
  },

      filters: { tags: 'Tags', untagged: 'No tags', excludedOnly: 'Only excluded from analytics' },
      selection: { field_addTags: 'Add tag', field_removeTags: 'Remove tag' },
      bulk: {
        title_addTags: 'Add tags',
        title_removeTags: 'Remove tags',
        tagsAddHint: 'The tags are added to the ones a transaction has, at most 10 each.',
        tagsNone: 'The selected transactions have no tags',
        errors: { tag_not_found: 'The tag was not found', tag_archived: 'The tag is archived' },
      },
      export: { column_tags: 'Tags' },
    },
  },
}
function merge(target, source) {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && target[key] !== null && typeof target[key] === 'object') merge(target[key], value)
    else target[key] = value
  }
}
for (const [lang, fragment] of Object.entries(ADD)) {
  const file = `messages/${lang}.json`
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
  const data = JSON.parse(src)
  merge(data, fragment)
  let out = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n'
  if (src.includes('\r\n')) out = out.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n')
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

Замінити `lib/api/rule-draft.ts` повністю на:

```ts
import { fromMinor, toMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { RuleConditionInput, RuleInput } from '@/lib/domain/rules/input'
import { MAX_VALUES, type Rule, type RuleType, type TextOp } from '@/lib/domain/rules/types'
import type { Locale } from '@/lib/i18n/config'
import { formatAmountInput, parseAmountInput } from '@/lib/utils/amount-input'

/** One condition as the editor holds it: the words and the amounts as typed. */
export type DraftCondition =
  | { kind: 'text'; op: TextOp; values: string }
  | { kind: 'account'; accountIds: string[] }
  | { kind: 'amount'; currency: string; min: string; max: string }

export interface RuleDraft {
  name: string
  type: RuleType
  active: boolean
  conditions: DraftCondition[]
  /** '' sets no category. */
  categoryId: string
  counterpartyId: string | null
  /** Tags the rule adds (S10t); [] adds none. */
  tagIds: string[]
}

/** An empty condition of a kind; an amount starts in `currency` (the company currency, '' while it loads). */
export function newCondition(kind: DraftCondition['kind'], currency: string): DraftCondition {
  if (kind === 'text') return { kind: 'text', op: 'contains', values: '' }
  if (kind === 'account') return { kind: 'account', accountIds: [] }
  return { kind: 'amount', currency, min: '', max: '' }
}

/** A new rule: an expense with one empty text condition (bank rules are mostly about expenses). */
export function emptyDraft(): RuleDraft {
  return { name: '', type: 'expense', active: true, conditions: [newCondition('text', '')], categoryId: '', counterpartyId: null, tagIds: [] }
}

/** The words of a text condition: split at commas, semicolons and line breaks, trimmed, empty ones dropped. */
export function splitValues(text: string): string[] {
  return text
    .split(/[,;\n]/)
    .map((v) => v.trim())
    .filter((v) => v !== '')
}

export function draftFromRule(rule: Rule, locale: Locale): RuleDraft {
  const amount = (minor: number | undefined) => (minor === undefined ? '' : formatAmountInput(fromMinor(minor), locale))
  return {
    name: rule.name,
    type: rule.type,
    active: rule.active,
    conditions: rule.conditions.map((c): DraftCondition => {
      if (c.kind === 'text') return { kind: 'text', op: c.op, values: c.values.join(', ') }
      if (c.kind === 'account') return { kind: 'account', accountIds: [...c.accountIds] }
      return { kind: 'amount', currency: c.currency, min: amount(c.minMinor), max: amount(c.maxMinor) }
    }),
    categoryId: rule.actions.categoryId ?? '',
    counterpartyId: rule.actions.counterpartyId ?? null,
    tagIds: [...(rule.actions.tagIds ?? [])],
  }
}

/** undefined for an empty field, null for text that is not an amount. */
function minorOf(text: string): number | undefined | null {
  if (text.trim() === '') return undefined
  const value = parseAmountInput(text)
  return value === null ? null : toMinor(value)
}

/** The request body of the draft, or null while it is not complete (the server checks everything again). */
export function draftToInput(d: RuleDraft): RuleInput | null {
  const name = d.name.trim()
  if (name === '' || d.conditions.length === 0) return null
  if (d.categoryId === '' && d.counterpartyId === null && d.tagIds.length === 0) return null
  const conditions: RuleConditionInput[] = []
  for (const c of d.conditions) {
    if (c.kind === 'text') {
      const values = splitValues(c.values)
      if (values.length === 0 || values.length > MAX_VALUES) return null
      conditions.push({ kind: 'text', op: c.op, values })
    } else if (c.kind === 'account') {
      if (c.accountIds.length === 0) return null
      conditions.push({ kind: 'account', accountIds: [...c.accountIds] })
    } else {
      const minMinor = minorOf(c.min)
      const maxMinor = minorOf(c.max)
      if (minMinor === null || maxMinor === null || (minMinor === undefined && maxMinor === undefined)) return null
      conditions.push({
        kind: 'amount',
        ...(c.currency === '' ? {} : { currency: c.currency }),
        ...(minMinor === undefined ? {} : { minMinor }),
        ...(maxMinor === undefined ? {} : { maxMinor }),
      })
    }
  }
  return {
    name,
    type: d.type,
    active: d.active,
    conditions,
    actions: {
      ...(d.categoryId === '' ? {} : { categoryId: d.categoryId }),
      ...(d.counterpartyId === null ? {} : { counterpartyId: d.counterpartyId }),
      ...(d.tagIds.length === 0 ? {} : { tagIds: [...d.tagIds] }),
    },
  }
}
```

Замінити `components/rules/RuleSummary.tsx` повністю на:

```tsx
'use client'

import { useTranslations } from 'next-intl'
import type { Counterparty } from '@/lib/domain/counterparties/types'
import { fromMinor } from '@/lib/domain/ledger/money'
import type { ListedRule, RuleCondition } from '@/lib/domain/rules/types'
import type { Tag } from '@/lib/domain/tags/types'
import { useCategoryName } from '@/lib/i18n/useCategoryName'
import { useFormatters } from '@/lib/i18n/useFormatters'
import type { Account, Category } from '@/types'

interface Props {
  rule: ListedRule
  accounts: Account[]
  categories: Category[]
  counterparties: Counterparty[]
  tags: Tag[]
}

/** One line under the rule's name: what it checks, then what it sets. A deleted account, category, counterparty or tag reads «(видалено)». */
export function RuleSummary({ rule, accounts, categories, counterparties, tags }: Props) {
  const t = useTranslations('settings.rules.summary')
  const { money } = useFormatters()
  const categoryName = useCategoryName()

  const condition = (c: RuleCondition): string => {
    if (c.kind === 'text') return t(c.op, { values: c.values.join(', ') })
    if (c.kind === 'account') {
      return t('account', { names: c.accountIds.map((id) => accounts.find((a) => a.id === id)?.name ?? t('missing')).join(', ') })
    }
    const min = c.minMinor === undefined ? null : money(fromMinor(c.minMinor), c.currency)
    const max = c.maxMinor === undefined ? null : money(fromMinor(c.maxMinor), c.currency)
    if (min !== null && max !== null) return t('amountRange', { min, max })
    return min !== null ? t('amountMin', { min }) : t('amountMax', { max: max ?? '' })
  }

  const { categoryId, counterpartyId, tagIds } = rule.actions
  const category = categoryId === undefined ? undefined : categories.find((c) => c.id === categoryId)
  const counterparty = counterpartyId === undefined ? undefined : counterparties.find((c) => c.id === counterpartyId)
  const actions: string[] = []
  if (categoryId !== undefined) actions.push(t('category', { name: category ? categoryName(category) : t('missing') }))
  if (counterpartyId !== undefined) actions.push(t('counterparty', { name: counterparty?.name ?? t('missing') }))
  if (tagIds !== undefined) actions.push(t('tags', { names: tagIds.map((id) => tags.find((x) => x.id === id)?.name ?? t('missing')).join(', ') }))

  return (
    <p className="text-xs text-ink-muted">
      {rule.conditions.map(condition).join(' · ')} → {actions.join(', ')}
    </p>
  )
}
```

```js
          tags: 'Dodaj tagi',
          tagsHint: 'Reguła tylko dodaje te tagi do istniejących, niczego nie zdejmuje.',
          checkResult: 'Operacje, którym reguła może coś dodać: {scanned}, pasuje: {matched}',
          incomplete: 'Aby zapisać, uzupełnij nazwę i wszystkie warunki oraz wybierz kategorię, kontrahenta lub tagi',
        },
        errors: { tagMissing: 'Nie znaleziono tagu', tagArchived: 'Tag jest w archiwum' },
      },
    },
  },
  en: {
    settings: {
      rules: {
        description: 'Category, counterparty and tags for new bank transactions by text, account and amount. A rule only fills empty fields and only adds tags.',
        unusableHint: 'The category, the counterparty or a tag was deleted or archived, or the category has another type',
        summary: { tags: 'tags {names}' },
        editor: {
          tags: 'Add tags',
          tagsHint: 'The rule only adds these tags to the ones a transaction has; it never removes any.',
          checkResult: 'Transactions the rule could add something to: {scanned}, matching: {matched}',
          incomplete: 'To save, fill in the name and every condition, and pick a category, a counterparty or tags',
        },
        errors: { tagMissing: 'The tag was not found', tagArchived: 'The tag is archived' },
      },
    },
  },
}
function merge(target, source) {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(source)) {
    if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) && target[key] !== null && typeof target[key] === 'object') merge(target[key], value)
    else target[key] = value
  }
}
for (const [lang, fragment] of Object.entries(ADD)) {
  const file = `messages/${lang}.json`
  const src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')
  const data = JSON.parse(src)
  merge(data, fragment)
  let out = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2) + '\n'
  if (src.includes('\r\n')) out = out.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n')
  fs.writeFileSync(file, out)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Створити `scripts/lib/check-tags.ts`:

```ts
// Pure part of scripts/check-tags.ts (S10t rollout guard). Unit-tested in Vitest.

/** One operation as the check reads it: its id and whatever is stored in `tags`. */
export interface TaggedDoc {
  id: string
  tags: unknown
}

/**
 * The operations whose `tags` hold anything but the ids of the company's tags: free text from before S10t, an entry that
 * is not a string, or a `tags` that is not a list. An absent or empty `tags` is fine. Such an operation could not be edited
 * once the ledger checks tag ids (`invalid_input` on `tags`).
 */
export function freeTextTagged(docs: readonly TaggedDoc[], tagIds: ReadonlySet<string>): string[] {
  return docs
    .filter(({ tags }) => {
      if (tags === undefined) return false
      if (!Array.isArray(tags)) return true
      return tags.some((t) => typeof t !== 'string' || !tagIds.has(t))
    })
    .map((d) => d.id)
}
```

Створити `scripts/check-tags.ts`:

```ts
// Read-only guard for the S10t rollout: before `tags` become ids of the «Теги» list, no operation may still carry free
// text there (an edit of such an operation would be refused once the ledger checks tag ids). Never writes anything.
//   npm run check:tags                                      over the emulator when FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST is set
//   npm run check:tags -- --project=finflow-e94b5           production (the flag is required; nothing is read without it)
//   --org=<id> limits the run to one company.
// Prints counts and operation ids only, never the text of a tag.
// Exit code: 0 all clear, 1 an operation carries something that is not a tag id or a read failed, 2 refused or bad arguments.
import fs from 'node:fs'
import path from 'node:path'
import { applicationDefault, cert, initializeApp } from 'firebase-admin/app'
import { getFirestore, type Firestore } from 'firebase-admin/firestore'
import { tagsCol, transactionsCol } from '@/lib/server/ledger/paths'
import { assertReadinessTarget, parseCheckJournalArgs } from './lib/check-journal-args'
import { freeTextTagged } from './lib/check-tags'

const PROJECT_ID = 'finflow-e94b5'
const EMULATOR_PROJECT_ID = 'demo-finflow'
/** Operation ids printed per company; the count is always complete. */
const SHOW_IDS = 10

function fail(message: string, code: number): never {
  console.error(message)
  process.exit(code)
}

function credential() {
  if (process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS) return applicationDefault()
  const dir = path.resolve('.secrets')
  const file = fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).find((f) => /^finflow-.*-firebase-adminsdk-.*\.json$/.test(f)) : undefined
  if (!file) fail('No credentials: set GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS or put the service-account JSON in .secrets/.', 2)
  return cert(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, file), 'utf8')))
}

function describeError(e: unknown): string {
  if (e instanceof Error) {
    const code = (e as { code?: unknown }).code
    return `${code !== undefined ? `code ${String(code)}` : 'error'}: ${e.message}`
  }
  return 'error'
}

async function checkOrg(db: Firestore, orgId: string): Promise<{ operations: number; offending: string[] }> {
  const tagIds = new Set((await tagsCol(db, orgId).select().get()).docs.map((d) => d.id))
  const snap = await transactionsCol(db, orgId).select('tags').get()
  const offending = freeTextTagged(snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, tags: d.get('tags') as unknown })), tagIds)
  return { operations: snap.size, offending }
}

async function main(): Promise<number> {
  const parsed = parseCheckJournalArgs(process.argv.slice(2))
  if (!parsed.ok) fail(parsed.message, 2)
  const { org, raw } = parsed.args

  const target = assertReadinessTarget({ emulator: !!process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST, args: raw })
  if (!target.ok) fail(target.message, 2)

  if (process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST) {
    initializeApp({ projectId: EMULATOR_PROJECT_ID })
    console.log(`target: Firestore EMULATOR (project ${EMULATOR_PROJECT_ID})`)
  } else {
    initializeApp({ credential: credential(), projectId: PROJECT_ID })
    console.log(`target: PRODUCTION (project ${PROJECT_ID}), read-only`)
  }
  const db = getFirestore()

  const orgIds = org ? [org] : (await db.collection('organizations').select().get()).docs.map((d) => d.id)
  console.log(`companies: ${orgIds.length}`)

  const notReady: string[] = []
  const errored: string[] = []
  let operations = 0
  for (const orgId of orgIds) {
    try {
      const r = await checkOrg(db, orgId)
      operations += r.operations
      if (r.offending.length > 0) {
        notReady.push(orgId)
        const shown = r.offending.slice(0, SHOW_IDS).join(', ')
        console.log(`org ${orgId}: NOT READY, ${r.offending.length} of ${r.operations} operation(s) carry tags that are not tag ids (${shown}${r.offending.length > SHOW_IDS ? ', …' : ''})`)
      } else if (r.operations > 0) {
        console.log(`org ${orgId}: ok (${r.operations} operation(s))`)
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`org ${orgId}: FAILED (${describeError(e)})`)
      errored.push(orgId)
    }
  }
  console.log(`checked ${orgIds.length - errored.length} company(ies), ${operations} operation(s); not ready: ${notReady.length}; errors: ${errored.length}`)
  if (notReady.length > 0) console.error(`NOT READY (${notReady.length}): ${notReady.join(', ')}`)
  if (errored.length > 0) console.error(`failed companies (${errored.length}): ${errored.join(', ')}`)
  if (notReady.length === 0 && errored.length === 0) console.log('READY: no operation carries free-text tags. Nothing was written.')
  return notReady.length > 0 || errored.length > 0 ? 1 : 0
}

main().then(
  (code) => process.exit(code),
  (e) => {
    console.error(`FAILED (${describeError(e)})`)
    process.exit(1)
  },
)
```

`package.json`: після рядка `    "check:journal": "node --conditions=react-server --import tsx scripts/check-journal-readiness.ts",` додати:

```json

```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the e2e spec**

Створити `tests/e2e/tags.spec.ts`:

```ts
// tests/e2e/tags.spec.ts
// Tags (S10t): the settings section, the form field (inline create too), bulk add and remove, the journal filter, a rule that
// tags what the fake bank imports (tests/e2e/helpers/eb-stub.mjs) and a flag change with its recalculation.
import { test, expect, type APIRequestContext, type Locator, type Page } from '@playwright/test'
import { registerViaUi, waitForShell, type RegisteredUser } from './helpers/app-ui'
import { dayFromToday, seedConnection, seedLinkedBankAccount } from './helpers/bank-seed'
import { readDoc, seedDoc } from './helpers/emulator-firestore'
import { seedLedgerAccount, seedLedgerOperation } from './helpers/ledger-seed'
import { todayDay } from '../../lib/utils/today'

const NEEDS_EMULATORS =
  'Needs the Auth + Firestore emulators. Run: firebase emulators:exec --only auth,firestore --project demo-finflow "npm run test:e2e"'
const ORIGIN = { Origin: 'http://localhost:3000' }

const rowsOf = (page: Page): Locator => page.locator('tbody').getByRole('row').filter({ has: page.getByRole('checkbox') })
const toast = (page: Page, text: string): Locator => page.locator('[data-sonner-toast]').filter({ hasText: text })
const formDialog = (page: Page): Locator => page.getByRole('dialog').filter({ has: page.locator('#tx-amount') })
const accountRow = (page: Page, name: string): Locator =>
  page.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'Рахунки' }).getByRole('listitem').filter({ hasText: name })

/** A tag in the exact shape the server stores (lib/domain/tags/doc.ts). No name key: these specs never create a second tag of that name. */
async function seedTag(request: APIRequestContext, orgId: string, id: string, name: string): Promise<void> {
  await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${orgId}/tags`, id, {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    name,
    color: 'sky',
    excludeFromAnalytics: false,
    archived: false,
    recalcPending: false,
    orgId,
    createdAt: new Date(),
    createdBy: 'e2e',
    updatedAt: new Date(),
  })
}

/** The stored balance of an account in minor units (seeded numbers and the ledger's increments are doubles). */
async function balanceMinor(request: APIRequestContext, orgId: string, accountId: string): Promise<number> {
  const raw = (await readDoc(request, `organizations/${orgId}/accounts/${accountId}`))?.balanceMinor as unknown as
    | { doubleValue?: number; integerValue?: string }
    | undefined
  return Number(raw?.doubleValue ?? raw?.integerValue)
}

/** A PLN company with one bank account of 1000 zł, on the journal. */
async function setup(page: Page, request: APIRequestContext, prefix: string): Promise<RegisteredUser> {
  const user = await registerViaUi(page, prefix, 'PLN')
  await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
  await seedLedgerAccount(request, user.orgId, 'acc-bank', { name: 'Konto PLN', balance: 1000, currency: 'PLN' })
  await expect(accountRow(page, 'Konto PLN')).toBeVisible()
  return user
}

async function addExpense(page: Page, amount: string, description: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Витрата', exact: true }).click()
  const form = formDialog(page)
  await form.locator('#tx-amount').fill(amount)
  await form.locator('#tx-desc').fill(description)
  await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Додати', exact: true }).click()
  await expect(form).toBeHidden()
}

test.describe('tags (emulators)', () => {
  test.skip(!process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST || !process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST, NEEDS_EMULATORS)

  test('a tag made in settings is given in the form, shows as a chip and filters the journal', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'tg-form')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/settings/tags`)
    await expect(page.getByText('Тегів ще немає')).toBeVisible()
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Додати тег' }).click()
    const editor = page.getByRole('dialog')
    await editor.locator('#tag-name').fill('ПДВ')
    await editor.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Рожевий' }).click()
    await editor.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Не враховувати в аналітиці' }).check()
    await editor.getByRole('button', { name: 'Зберегти', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(toast(page, 'Тег збережено')).toBeVisible()
    await expect(editor).toBeHidden()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-row')).toContainText('ПДВ')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-row').getByTestId('tag-excluded')).toHaveText('Не враховується в аналітиці')

    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/transactions`)
    await expect(accountRow(page, 'Konto PLN')).toBeVisible()
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Витрата', exact: true }).click()
    const form = formDialog(page)
    await form.locator('#tx-amount').fill('230')
    await form.locator('#tx-desc').fill('VAT')
    await form.locator('#tx-tags').fill('пдв')
    await form.getByRole('option', { name: /ПДВ/ }).click()
    await expect(form.getByTestId('tag-picked')).toContainText('ПДВ')
    await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Додати', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(form).toBeHidden()
    await addExpense(page, '50', 'Kawa')

    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(2)
    await expect(rowsOf(page).filter({ hasText: 'VAT' }).getByTestId('tag-chip')).toContainText('ПДВ')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-chip')).toHaveCount(1)

    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Додаткові фільтри' }).click()
    const filter = page.getByTestId('journal-tag-filter')
    await filter.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'ПДВ' }).check()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/[?&]tg=/)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(1)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toContainText('VAT')
    await filter.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'ПДВ' }).uncheck()
    await filter.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Без тегів' }).check()
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(1)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toContainText('Kawa')
    await filter.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Без тегів' }).uncheck()
    await filter.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Лише не враховані в аналітиці' }).check()
    await expect(page).toHaveURL(/[?&]ex=1/)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(1)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toContainText('VAT')
    // The tag keeps the expense out of the analytics, not out of the balance: 1000 − 230 − 50.
    expect(await balanceMinor(request, user.orgId, 'acc-bank')).toBe(72_000)
  })

  test('a new tag can be created right from the form field', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'tg-inline')
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Витрата', exact: true }).click()
    const form = formDialog(page)
    await form.locator('#tx-amount').fill('1000')
    await form.locator('#tx-tags').fill('Bankomat')
    await form.getByRole('option', { name: 'Створити «Bankomat»' }).click()
    await expect(form.getByTestId('tag-picked')).toContainText('Bankomat')
    await form.getByRole('button', { name: 'Додати', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(form).toBeHidden()
    await expect(rowsOf(page).getByTestId('tag-chip')).toContainText('Bankomat')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/settings/tags`)
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-row')).toContainText('Bankomat')
  })

  test('bulk: add a tag to the selected operations, then take it off', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'tg-bulk')
    await seedTag(request, user.orgId, 'tag-q4', 'Q4')
    await seedLedgerOperation(request, user.orgId, 'op-1', { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-bank', amount: 10, currency: 'PLN', description: 'Op 1' })
    await seedLedgerOperation(request, user.orgId, 'op-2', { type: 'expense', accountId: 'acc-bank', amount: 20, currency: 'PLN', description: 'Op 2' })
    await page.reload()
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(2)
    const bar = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Вибрані операції' })
    const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog')

    for (const i of [0, 1]) await rowsOf(page).nth(i).getByRole('checkbox').check()
    await bar.getByRole('button', { name: 'Змінити дані' }).click()
    await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Додати тег' }).click()
    await dialog.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Теги' }).fill('q4')
    await dialog.getByRole('option', { name: /Q4/ }).click()
    await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Застосувати' }).click()
    await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-chip')).toHaveCount(2)

    for (const i of [0, 1]) await rowsOf(page).nth(i).getByRole('checkbox').check()
    await bar.getByRole('button', { name: 'Змінити дані' }).click()
    await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Зняти тег' }).click()
    await dialog.getByRole('combobox', { name: 'Теги' }).click()
    await dialog.getByRole('option', { name: /Q4/ }).click()
    await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Застосувати' }).click()
    await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-chip')).toHaveCount(0)
  })

  test('a rule that adds a tag tags what the fake bank imports', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await registerViaUi(page, 'tg-rule', 'PLN')
    await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
    await seedLedgerAccount(request, user.orgId, 'acc-bank', { name: 'Fake Bank PLN', balance: 0, currency: 'PLN' })
    await seedTag(request, user.orgId, 'tag-fuel', 'Paliwo firmowe')
    // A rule in the exact shape the server stores (lib/domain/rules/doc.ts).
    await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${user.orgId}/rules`, 'rule-fuel', {
      schemaVersion: 1,
      orgId: user.orgId,
      name: 'Orlen',
      type: 'expense',
      active: true,
      order: 0,
      conditions: [{ kind: 'text', op: 'contains', values: ['orlen'] }],
      actions: { tagIds: ['tag-fuel'] },
      createdAt: new Date(),
      createdBy: 'e2e',
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    })
    await seedConnection(request, user.orgId, dayFromToday(60))
    await seedLinkedBankAccount(request, user.orgId, 'acc-bank')
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/transactions`)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(3)
    const fuel = rowsOf(page).filter({ hasText: 'Orlen' })
    await expect(fuel.getByTestId('tag-chip')).toContainText('Paliwo firmowe')
    await expect(fuel.getByTestId('rule-chip')).toHaveText('за правилом: Orlen')
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-chip')).toHaveCount(1)
  })

  test('switching «Не враховувати» on a used tag asks first, recalculates with progress and leaves the balances alone', async ({ page, request }) => {
    const user = await setup(page, request, 'tg-recalc')
    await seedLedgerAccount(request, user.orgId, 'acc-cash', { name: 'Kasa', balance: 0, currency: 'PLN', type: 'cash' })
    // The real ledger writes these (rollups and audit included): the browser context carries the session cookie.
    const created = await page.request.post(`/api/orgs/${user.orgId}/tags`, { headers: ORIGIN, data: { name: 'Bankomat' } })
    expect(created.status()).toBe(201)
    const { tag } = (await created.json()) as { tag: { id: string } }
    // The cash machine: 1000 out of the bank, 1000 into the cash box, both with the tag.
    for (const [type, accountId] of [['expense', 'acc-bank'], ['income', 'acc-cash']] as const) {
      const res = await page.request.post(`/api/orgs/${user.orgId}/transactions`, {
        headers: ORIGIN,
        data: { type, accountId, amountMinor: 100_000, paymentDate: todayDay(), tags: [tag.id] },
      })
      expect(res.status()).toBe(201)
    }

    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/settings/tags`)
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Редагувати «Bankomat»' }).click()
    const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog')
    await dialog.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Не враховувати в аналітиці' }).check()
    await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Зберегти', exact: true }).click()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-count')).toHaveText('Буде перераховано операцій: 2. Залишки рахунків не зміняться.')
    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Перерахувати' }).click()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-state')).toHaveText('Готово. Переглянуто операцій: 2')
    await page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-close').click()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-row').getByTestId('tag-excluded')).toBeVisible()
    await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-pending')).toHaveCount(0)

    // The balances did not move: the bank paid out 1000, the cash box got 1000.
    expect(await balanceMinor(request, user.orgId, 'acc-bank')).toBe(0)
    expect(await balanceMinor(request, user.orgId, 'acc-cash')).toBe(100_000)
    await page.goto(`/${user.orgId}/transactions?ex=1`)
    await expect(rowsOf(page)).toHaveCount(2)
  })
})
```

```ts
import { mkdirSync, readdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test'
import { registerViaUi, waitForShell } from '../e2e/helpers/app-ui'
import { replaceDoc, seedDoc } from '../e2e/helpers/emulator-firestore'
import { seedLedgerAccount, seedLedgerOperation } from '../e2e/helpers/ledger-seed'
import pl from '../../messages/pl.json'
import uk from '../../messages/uk.json'

// S10t: the tags UI (settings/tags with the editor, the flag confirmation and the recalculation, the form field, the
// journal chips and the tag filter) at 1440 and 390 px in uk and pl. Files: tags-<state>-<desktop|phone>-<uk|pl>.png.
const OUT_DIR = join(process.cwd(), 'ui-shots')
const VIEWPORTS = [
  { form: 'desktop', width: 1440, height: 900 },
  { form: 'phone', width: 390, height: 844 },
] as const
const LOCALES = [
  ['uk', uk],
  ['pl', pl],
] as const
const LONG = 'Koszty reprezentacyjne i delegacje zagraniczne'

/** A tag in the exact shape the server stores (lib/domain/tags/doc.ts). */
function tagDoc(orgId: string, name: string, color: string, flags: { excludeFromAnalytics?: boolean; archived?: boolean; recalcPending?: boolean } = {}) {
  return {
    schemaVersion: 1,
    name,
    color,
    excludeFromAnalytics: flags.excludeFromAnalytics === true,
    archived: flags.archived === true,
    recalcPending: flags.recalcPending === true,
    orgId,
    createdAt: new Date(),
    createdBy: 'shots',
    updatedAt: new Date(),
  }
}

test.describe('tags shots', () => {
  test.skip(
    !process.env.FIREBASE_AUTH_EMULATOR_HOST || !process.env.FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST,
    'Run with `npm run ui:shots` (it starts the Auth + Firestore emulators)',
  )

  test.beforeAll(() => {
    mkdirSync(OUT_DIR, { recursive: true })
    for (const old of readdirSync(OUT_DIR)) {
      if (old.startsWith('tags-') && old.endsWith('.png')) rmSync(join(OUT_DIR, old), { force: true })
    }
  })

  for (const [locale, messages] of LOCALES) {
    test(`tags in ${locale}`, async ({ page, request, context }) => {
      test.setTimeout(300_000)
      await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 900 })
      const user = await registerViaUi(page, `tgshots${locale}`, 'PLN')
      await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
      const org = user.orgId
      const m = messages.settings.tags

      await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-main', { name: 'Konto główne', balance: 25000, currency: 'PLN' })
      await seedLedgerAccount(request, org, 'acc-cash', { name: 'Kasa', balance: 1500, currency: 'PLN', type: 'cash' })
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/categories`, 'cat-fuel', { name: 'Paliwo', type: 'expense', color: '#22c55e', orgId: org, createdAt: new Date() })
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags`, 'tag-vat', tagDoc(org, 'ПДВ', 'rose', { excludeFromAnalytics: true }))
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags`, 'tag-atm', tagDoc(org, 'Bankomat', 'amber', { excludeFromAnalytics: true, recalcPending: true }))
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags`, 'tag-q4', tagDoc(org, 'Q4', 'sky'))
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags`, 'tag-long', tagDoc(org, LONG, 'violet'))
      await seedDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags`, 'tag-old', tagDoc(org, 'Stare', 'slate', { archived: true }))

      const main = { accountId: 'acc-main', currency: 'PLN' } as const
      await seedLedgerOperation(request, org, 'op-1', { ...main, type: 'income', amount: 230, date: '2026-10-27', description: 'Zwrot VAT', tags: ['tag-vat'], excluded: true })
      await seedLedgerOperation(request, org, 'op-2', { ...main, type: 'expense', amount: 230, date: '2026-10-27', description: 'VAT', tags: ['tag-vat'], excluded: true })
      await seedLedgerOperation(request, org, 'op-3', { ...main, type: 'expense', amount: 1000, date: '2026-10-26', description: 'Bankomat', tags: ['tag-atm'], excluded: true })
      await seedLedgerOperation(request, org, 'op-4', { type: 'income', accountId: 'acc-cash', currency: 'PLN', amount: 1000, date: '2026-10-26', description: 'Wpłata do kasy', tags: ['tag-atm'], excluded: true })
      await seedLedgerOperation(request, org, 'op-5', { ...main, type: 'expense', amount: 212.4, date: '2026-10-25', description: 'ORLEN Stacja 1234', categoryId: 'cat-fuel', tags: ['tag-q4', 'tag-long', 'tag-old'] })

      if (locale !== 'uk') await context.addCookies([{ name: 'NEXT_LOCALE', value: locale, url: 'http://localhost:3000' }])

      const noHorizontalScroll = async (what: string): Promise<void> => {
        const over = await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth)
        expect(over, `${what}: the page scrolls sideways by ${over}px`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0)
      }
      const dialogFits = async (what: string): Promise<void> => {
        const box = await page.getByRole('dialog').boundingBox()
        const vp = page.viewportSize()
        expect(box, `${what}: no dialog`).not.toBeNull()
        if (box && vp) {
          expect(box.x, `${what}: dialog left`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
          expect(box.y, `${what}: dialog top`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
          expect(box.x + box.width, `${what}: dialog right`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(vp.width + 0.5)
          expect(box.y + box.height, `${what}: dialog bottom`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(vp.height + 0.5)
        }
      }

      for (const viewport of VIEWPORTS) {
        await page.setViewportSize({ width: viewport.width, height: viewport.height })
        const shot = async (state: string): Promise<void> => {
          await page.screenshot({ path: join(OUT_DIR, `tags-${state}-${viewport.form}-${locale}.png`), animations: 'disabled' })
        }
        const dialog = page.getByRole('dialog')
        // «Перерахунок не завершено» again for this viewport: the previous one finished it.
        await replaceDoc(request, `organizations/${org}/tags/tag-atm`, tagDoc(org, 'Bankomat', 'amber', { excludeFromAnalytics: true, recalcPending: true }))

        await page.goto(`/${org}/settings/tags`)
        await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
        await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-row')).toHaveCount(5)
        await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-pending')).toBeVisible()
        await noHorizontalScroll('list')
        await shot('list')

        // The editor of a used tag, then the question a flag change asks.
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: m.edit.replace('{name}', 'Q4') }).click()
        await expect(dialog).toBeVisible()
        await dialogFits('editor')
        await shot('editor')
        await dialog.getByRole('switch', { name: m.editor.exclude }).check()
        await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: messages.common.save, exact: true }).click()
        await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-count')).toBeVisible()
        await dialogFits('confirm')
        await shot('confirm')
        await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: messages.common.cancel, exact: true }).click()
        await expect(page.locator('#tag-name')).toBeVisible()
        await page.keyboard.press('Escape')
        await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()

        // «Продовжити»: the recalculation dialog runs to the end.
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: m.recalcContinue }).click()
        await expect(page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-state')).toContainText(m.recalc.done.split('{scanned}')[0].trim())
        await dialogFits('recalc')
        await shot('recalc-done')
        await page.getByTestId('tag-recalc-close').click()
        await expect(dialog).toBeHidden()

        // The journal: chips (a phone shows them under the account) and the tag filter.
        await page.goto(`/${org}/transactions`)
        await waitForShell(page, user.companyName)
        await expect(page.getByTestId(viewport.form === 'phone' ? 'tag-chip-phone' : 'tag-chip').first()).toBeVisible()
        await noHorizontalScroll('journal')
        await shot('journal-chips')
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: messages.journal.filters.more }).click()
        await expect(page.getByTestId('journal-tag-filter')).toBeVisible()
        await noHorizontalScroll('journal filter')
        await shot('journal-filter')

        // The operation form with two tags chosen.
        await page.getByRole('button', { name: messages.shell.header.expense }).click()
        const form = page.getByRole('dialog').filter({ has: page.locator('#tx-amount') })
        await form.locator('#tx-amount').fill('89,99')
        await form.locator('#tx-tags').fill('q4')
        await form.getByRole('option', { name: /Q4/ }).click()
        await form.locator('#tx-tags').fill('ban')
        await form.getByRole('option', { name: /Bankomat/ }).click()
        await form.locator('#tx-tags').blur()
        await form.locator('#tx-tags').scrollIntoViewIfNeeded()
        await dialogFits('form')
        await shot('form-tags')
        await page.keyboard.press('Escape')
        const discard = page.getByRole('dialog').filter({ hasText: messages.forms.transaction.discardTitle })
        await discard.getByRole('button', { name: messages.forms.transaction.discardConfirm, exact: true }).click()
        await expect(form).toBeHidden()
      }
    })
  }
})
```


3. У `## Firestore Data Model (S00, S02)` у списку `subcollections:` після `` `rules` (S10, written by the server, readable by every member)`` додати ``, `tags` (S10t, written by the server, readable by every member), `tagKeys` (S10t, server only; name keys)``.
4. У кінець файлу (після блоку `## Rules (S10)`) додати:

```
## Tags (S10t)

Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-29-s10t-tags-design.md`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-29-s10t-tags.md`.

- **Data:** `organizations/{org}/tags/{tagId}` `{ schemaVersion: 1, name (1-50), color (a `TAG_COLORS` key: slate, sky, emerald, amber, rose, violet), excludeFromAnalytics, archived, recalcPending, orgId, createdAt, createdBy, updatedAt }`, written only by `lib/server/tags/write.ts` (one Admin transaction with the name key `tagKeys/name_{nameKeyText}` and the audit entry `tag.create|update`), readable by every member; `tagKeys` is closed to clients. At most `MAX_TAGS` = 100 per company (409 `limit_reached`, checked in the create transaction); a taken name is 409 `duplicate` with `field: 'name'` and `existingId`. No delete, only `archived`. Nothing is created automatically. The browser reads the list live (`useTags`, `lib/firebase/tags.ts`); `GET /tags` exists too.
- **Operations:** `tags` are tag ids (at most 10, none twice, `isId`). The ledger write reads every tag of the new state inside its transaction: a tag given anew must exist (422 `tag_not_found`) and not be archived (422 `tag_archived`); tags the operation already had are not checked, so an edit of something else or taking an archived tag off never fails. `excluded: true` (stored only when true, never accepted from a request) when at least one tag has `excludeFromAnalytics`; then `contributions` writes the same fields to `x_cash_*`, `x_cashcat_*`, `x_pl_*` and `balanceEffects` ignores the flag. `verifyLedger` reports `excludedDiffs`; `rebuildLedger` rewrites the flag and the `x_` documents. The journal's balance after a row reads the `x_cash_` days too. The Cash Flow and P&L pages skip `excluded` operations (`lib/utils/analytics.ts`); reports with a tag filter and an «excluded» line are S12. Tags are not bank-locked.
- **Flag change:** `GET /tags/{id}/impact` counts the operations (`count()`); a `PATCH` that changes the flag of a used tag sets `recalcPending` in the same transaction and answers `recalc: true`; the browser loops `POST /tags/{id}/recalculate { cursor? }` (`recalculateTag` in `lib/api/tags-client.ts`): 100 operations a step in id order (`array-contains`, no new index), each through `refreshExcluded` (`lib/server/ledger/write.ts`, one transaction, rollups only, audit `tx.update` with `via: 'tag'`); the step that reads the last ones clears `recalcPending`. Repeating a step is safe. A tag left with `recalcPending` shows «Перерахунок не завершено» and «Продовжити» in settings.
- **Journal:** bulk `patch: { addTagIds?, removeTagIds? }` (1-10 ids each, never with other fields) runs `retagTransaction` per operation (`mergeTags`: takes off, then adds while below 10; `updateTransaction` with `expectVersion`; an unchanged list writes nothing). Filters `tg` (ids, `_none` = no tags) and `ex=1` (only excluded) work in memory. Chips per row (`tag-chip`, on a phone `tag-chip-phone`), CSV column «Теги» (names joined by `, `).
- **Rules:** action `tagIds` (1-5) next to the category and the counterparty. Tags are added, never removed: the union of the operation's tags and the tags of every matching rule, in rule order, at most 10. One missing or archived tag makes the whole tag action «неробоче»; an edit checks only the tags it adds. Import writes again without the rule on `tag_not_found`/`tag_archived`. «Застосувати до наявних» counts `tags`; «Перевірити» counts operations that lack a tag of the rule too.
- **UI:** settings section «Теги» (`settings/tags`, slug `tags`, every member sees it, editing needs `data.write`); `components/tags/{TagDot,TagPicker,TagChips,TagEditor,TagRecalcDialog}.tsx`; the form field «Теги» (`components/forms/transaction/TagsField.tsx`, a new tag can be created from it) is owned by the form now (`FormOwnedDraft` keeps only project and files out). The colours are the `--color-tag-*` tokens (`lib/ui/tag-colors.ts`).
- **Rollout guard:** `npm run check:tags -- --project=finflow-e94b5` (read-only; refuses production without the flag) lists the operations whose `tags` are not tag ids; it must report none before the deploy.
- **Tests:** unit `tests/unit/domain/tags/*`, `tests/unit/domain/ledger/excluded.test.ts`, `tests/unit/domain/rules/tags.test.ts`, `tests/unit/api/tags/routes.test.ts`, `tests/unit/api/tags-client.test.ts`, `tests/unit/server/tags/http.test.ts`, `tests/unit/scripts/check-tags.test.ts`; integration `tests/integration/{tags-write,ledger-tags,tags-recalc,journal-tags,rules-tags}.test.ts`; rules in `tests/rules/`; e2e `tests/e2e/tags.spec.ts`; screenshots `tests/ui-shots/tags.spec.ts` (`playwright.shots.config.ts`).
```

- `### Підсумок перевірок (дата, реально виміряно)`: числа з кроку 2 для `typecheck`, `lint` (errors/warnings), `check:server-imports`, unit, rules, integration, build, e2e, з порівнянням із S10 (unit 2126/147, rules 131/2, integration 250/24, e2e 119);
- `### Не перевірено`: реальні дані production (`check:tags` ще не запускався на production), швидкість перерахунку на production, поведінка на телефоні в реальному браузері;
- `### Known follow-ups (S10t)`: дві швидкі зміни прапорця з двох вкладок (перший цикл, закінчившись, знімає `recalcPending`, поки другий ще йде; прапорець булевий, без версії); скасування видалення чи масової зміни, що повертає архівний тег, відмовляє (`tag_archived`), як з архівним контрагентом; залишок після рядка журналу читає ще один діапазон агрегатів (`x_cash_`): мінімальне читання на рахунок на сторінку; звіти з фільтром за тегами й рядком «Виключено з аналітики» (S12); лічильники по тегах (S12); операція з довільним текстом у `tags` (якщо `check:tags` колись таку знайде) не редагується, поки її теги не виправлено; перерахунок однієї операції це одна транзакція, тож тисячі операцій ідуть хвилини; кнопки зняття тегу в пікері 20 px (для дотику рекомендовано 44 px); видалення тегів немає (за спекою); планувальник міграції S05 (`scripts/lib/plan-ledger-v2.ts`, уже застосований) досі обрізає старі теги до 20 рядків по 50 символів і в коментарі посилається на межі `parseTransactionInput`, які S10t змінив (тепер лише id тегів): повторний запуск міграції на компанії зі старими тегами дав би операції, які `check:tags` покаже як нередаговані.

## Після реалізації (веде контролер, не субагент)

1. Огляд усієї гілки opus-рев'юером (свіжий контекст, еталон: спека і цей план), виправлення знахідок з тестами.
2. Візуальна перевірка: `npx firebase emulators:exec --only auth,firestore --project demo-finflow "npx playwright test --config playwright.shots.config.ts tests/ui-shots/tags.spec.ts"` і перегляд усіх `ui-shots/tags-*.png` на 1440 і 390 px українською й польською (довгі польські підписи, діалоги на телефоні, чипи й крапки кольору, мітка «Перерахунок не завершено»); після змін оболонки чи токенів також `npm run ui:shots`.
3. `git push -u origin s10t-tags`, чернетка PR (тіло англійською за зразком PR #17-#19, у кінці `🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)`), зелений CI (job `ci`). Якщо в CI падає e2e, якого не видно локально, спершу розібрати його з артефактів CI (звіт Playwright, трейси, скриншоти), знайти причину, виправити з тестом і перезапустити; не перезапускати наосліп.
4. Після зеленого CI, з окремого «ок» власника: на production лише читання `npm run check:tags -- --project=finflow-e94b5` (крок 1 «Розгортання» спеки). Якщо є операції з непорожніми `tags`, зупинитись і розібратись окремо.
5. **Зупинка і запит явного «ок» власника на злиття.** Далі кожен крок розгортання за окремим «ок»:
   1. Злиття PR і `firebase deploy --only firestore:rules --project finflow-e94b5 --account rekrutacja.gd523@gmail.com`. Нових індексів немає; міграції даних немає (відсутнє `excluded` це `false`).
   2. Vercel production READY; перевірка без входу: `GET /api/orgs/x/tags` дає `401`; `verifyLedger` лише читання на production (власник: `POST /api/orgs/{org}/ledger/rebuild?apply=0`).
   3. Власник створює свої теги, тегує пари операцій (ПДВ, банкомат і каса), дивиться чипи й фільтр у журналі, перевіряє, що баланси рахунків не змінились, а Cash Flow і P&L не показують виключених операцій.
   4. Оновити пам'ять і хаб в Obsidian.
